📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: International Immunopharmacology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-17  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.intimp.2026.117694  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 192, 117694
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

含Ii-Key的HPV16/18 E7表位肽疫苗促进树突状细胞激活和T细胞免疫,在小鼠HPV16肿瘤模型中表现出更强的肿瘤控制效果,联合PD-1阻断剂效果更佳。

An Ii-Key-containing HPV16/18 E7 epitope peptide vaccine promotes dendritic cell activation and T-cell immunity in a murine HPV16 tumor model, with greater tumor control upon PD-1 blockade.
作者： Zi-Xuan Li, Liang-Liang Shen, Xiao-Ning Wang, Hu Ren, Chun Cui, Xiao-Qian Tao, Xiao-Hui Yao, Yu-Ji Wang, Jin Qiu, Yu Lu
摘要中文翻译背景 针对HPV E6/E7癌蛋白的治疗性疫苗在宫颈癌免疫治疗中具有广阔前景,但大多数候选药物仍受限于抗原呈递效率不足和T细胞反应不理想。Ii-Key策略通过将不变链(Ii)核心模体与表位肽连接,以促进MHC II类分子的装载,可能通过增强CD4+ T辅助细胞反应来克服这一限制。方法 使用免疫信息学工具预测了HPV16/18 E7表位肽,这些表位肽被小鼠H-2 I/II类分子限制。通过流式细胞术、ELISA和Western blotting,在体外利用骨髓来源的树突状细胞(BMDC)刺激实验中合成并评估了Ii-Key修饰的MHC II类肽(LRMK-PEG2-表位)和未修饰的MHC I类肽。通过淋巴器官免疫表型分析和血浆细胞因子谱分析,在C57BL/6小鼠中评估了其体内免疫原性。在早期干预的皮下TC-1肿瘤模型(HPV16 E6/E7阳性)中测试了抗肿瘤活性,通过组织病理学分析和肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞的流式细胞术分析进行评估。还评估了治疗与抗PD-1单克隆抗体的联合治疗效果。结果 肽池促进了BMDC成熟,表现为CD80+CD86+和I-A/I-E+ BMDC频率增加、NF-κB通路激活以及促炎细胞因子分泌增加,这些指标表明树突状细胞激活。在TC-1模型中,肽治疗抑制了肿瘤生长并定性减少了肿瘤Ki67染色,伴随肿瘤内CD4+和CD8+ T细胞频率增加的非显著趋势。与抗PD-1联合使用可实现比单用更佳的肿瘤控制效果。通过组织病理学和血清生化评估的安全性分析表明,在所测试的条件和观察期内耐受性良好。结论 含Ii-Key的HPV16/18 E7肽制剂促进了树突状细胞激活和T细胞免疫,在早期干预的TC-1模型中表现出可接受的耐受性下的抗肿瘤活性。与抗PD-1联合使用比单用更能有效控制肿瘤,但这种比较并未证明其具有加成或协同效应。这些临床前研究结果支持进一步评估该制剂,包括直接抗原呈递实验和匹配的未修饰MHC-II肽对照。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 针对HPV E6/E7癌蛋白的治疗性疫苗在宫颈癌免疫治疗中具有广阔前景,但大多数候选药物仍受限于抗原呈递效率不足和T细胞反应不理想。
设计与对象
Ii-Key策略通过将不变链(Ii)核心模体与表位肽连接,以促进MHC II类分子的装载,可能通过增强CD4+ T辅助细胞反应来克服这一限制。使用免疫信息学工具预测了HPV16/18 E7表位肽,这些表位肽被小鼠H-2 I/II类分子限制。通过淋巴器官免疫表型分析和血浆细胞因子谱分析,在C57BL/6小鼠中评估了其体内免疫原性。
核心方法
通过流式细胞术、ELISA和Western blotting,在体外利用骨髓来源的树突状细胞(BMDC)刺激实验中合成并评估了Ii-Key修饰的MHC II类肽(LRMK-PEG2-表位)和未修饰的MHC I类肽。在早期干预的皮下TC-1肿瘤模型(HPV16 E6/E7阳性)中测试了抗肿瘤活性,通过组织病理学分析和肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞的流式细胞术分析进行评估。
主要结果
肽池促进了BMDC成熟,表现为CD80+CD86+和I-A/I-E+ BMDC频率增加、NF-κB通路激活以及促炎细胞因子分泌增加,这些指标表明树突状细胞激活。在TC-1模型中,肽治疗抑制了肿瘤生长并定性减少了肿瘤Ki67染色,伴随肿瘤内CD4+和CD8+ T细胞频率增加的非显著趋势。
解释与创新
含Ii-Key的HPV16/18 E7肽制剂促进了树突状细胞激活和T细胞免疫,在早期干预的TC-1模型中表现出可接受的耐受性下的抗肿瘤活性。与抗PD-1联合使用可实现比单用更佳的肿瘤控制效果,但这种比较并未证明其具有加成或协同效应。
科研与公卫意义
这些临床前研究结果支持进一步评估该制剂,包括直接抗原呈递实验和匹配的未修饰MHC-II肽对照。结果表明,通过增强抗原呈递和T细胞反应,可能提高治疗性疫苗在HPV相关癌症中的疗效。
局限与证据强度
通过组织病理学和血清生化评估的安全性分析表明,在所测试的条件和观察期内耐受性良好。所提供的证据未报告长期安全性和有效性数据,也未提供关于人体试验或更广泛应用于其他HPV类型的信息。

An Ii-Key-containing HPV16/18 E7 epitope peptide vaccine promotes dendritic cell activation and T-cell immunity in a murine HPV16 tumor model, with greater tumor control upon PD-1 blockade.

Authors: Zi-Xuan Li, Liang-Liang Shen, Xiao-Ning Wang, Hu Ren, Chun Cui, Xiao-Qian Tao, Xiao-Hui Yao, Yu-Ji Wang, Jin Qiu, Yu Lu
Original abstract (English)
Background Therapeutic vaccines targeting human papillomavirus (HPV) E6/E7 oncoproteins represent a promising strategy for cervical cancer immunotherapy, yet most candidates remain constrained by insufficient antigen presentation efficiency and suboptimal T-cell responses. The Ii-Key strategy, which links the invariant chain (Ii) core motif to epitope peptides to facilitate MHC class II loading, may overcome this limitation by amplifying CD4 + T helper responses. Methods HPV16/18 E7 epitopes restricted by murine H-2 class I/II molecules were predicted using immunoinformatics tools. Ii-Key-modified MHC class II peptides (LRMK-PEG2-epitope) and unmodified MHC class I peptides were synthesized and evaluated in vitro using bone marrow-derived dendritic cell (BMDC) stimulation assays with flow cytometry, ELISA, and western blotting. In vivo immunogenicity was assessed in C57BL/6 mice through immune phenotyping of lymphoid organs and plasma cytokine profiling. Anti-tumor activity was tested in an early-intervention subcutaneous TC-1 tumor model (HPV16 E6/E7-positive) with histopathological and flow cytometric analyses of tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes. Combination treatment with anti-PD-1 monoclonal antibody was also examined. Results The peptide pool promoted BMDC maturation, as evidenced by increased frequencies of CD80 + CD86 + and I-A/I-E + BMDCs, NF-κB pathway activation, and increased proinflammatory cytokine secretion; these readouts indicate DC activation. In C57BL/6 mice, peptide immunization induced DC activation in draining lymph nodes, enhanced CD4 + and CD8 + T-cell activation (CD8 + increase significant only in non-tumor-bearing mice), reduced Treg proportions in tumor-bearing mice (with a non-significant decline in non-tumor-bearing mice), elicited direct ex vivo splenocyte cytotoxicity against TC-1 cells, and altered memory-phenotype T-cell frequencies, increasing TCM frequency in non-tumor-bearing mice and TEM frequency in tumor-bearing mice. In the TC-1 model, peptide treatment suppressed tumor growth and qualitatively reduced tumor Ki67 staining, accompanied by non-significant trends toward increased intratumoral CD4 + and CD8 + T-cell frequencies. Combination with anti-PD-1 produced greater tumor control than either monotherapy. Safety assessments by histopathology and serum biochemistry indicated acceptable tolerability under the conditions and observation period tested. Conclusions The Ii-Key-containing HPV16/18 E7 peptide formulation promoted dendritic cell activation and T-cell immunity, achieving anti-tumor activity in an early-intervention TC-1 model with acceptable tolerability. The combination with PD-1 blockade produced greater tumor control than either monotherapy; this comparison does not establish additivity or synergy. These preclinical findings support further evaluation of the formulation incorporating direct antigen-presentation assays and matched unmodified MHC-II peptide controls.

Question and background
Background Therapeutic vaccines targeting human papillomavirus (HPV) E6/E7 oncoproteins represent a promising strategy for cervical cancer immunotherapy, yet most candidates remain constrained by insufficient antigen presentation efficiency and suboptimal T-cell responses.
Design and population
The Ii-Key strategy, which links the invariant chain (Ii) core motif to epitope peptides to facilitate MHC class II loading, may overcome this limitation by amplifying CD4 + T helper responses. HPV16/18 E7 epitopes restricted by murine H-2 class I/II molecules were predicted using immunoinformatics tools. In vivo immunogenicity was assessed in C57BL/6 mice through immune phenotyping of lymphoid organs and plasma cytokine profiling.
Core methods
Ii-Key-modified MHC class II peptides (LRMK-PEG2-epitope) and unmodified MHC class I peptides were synthesized and evaluated in vitro using bone marrow-derived dendritic cell (BMDC) stimulation assays with flow cytometry, ELISA, and western blotting. Anti-tumor activity was tested in an early-intervention subcutaneous TC-1 tumor model (HPV16 E6/E7-positive) with histopathological and flow cytometric analyses of tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes.
Main results
The peptide pool promoted BMDC maturation, as evidenced by increased frequencies of CD80 + CD86 + and I-A/I-E + BMDCs, NF-κB pathway activation, and increased proinflammatory cytokine secretion; these readouts indicate DC activation. In the TC-1 model, peptide treatment suppressed tumor growth and qualitatively reduced tumor Ki67 staining, accompanied by non-significant trends toward increased intratumoral CD4 + and CD8 + T-cell frequencies.
Interpretation and novelty
The Ii-Key-containing HPV16/18 E7 peptide formulation promoted dendritic cell activation and T-cell immunity, achieving anti-tumor activity in an early-intervention TC-1 model with acceptable tolerability. Combination with anti-PD-1 produced greater tumor control than either monotherapy; this comparison does not establish additivity or synergy.
Scientific and public-health significance
These preclinical findings support further evaluation of the formulation incorporating direct antigen-presentation assays and matched unmodified MHC-II peptide controls. The results suggest potential for improving therapeutic vaccines against HPV-associated cancers by enhancing antigen presentation and T-cell responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
Safety assessments by histopathology and serum biochemistry indicated acceptable tolerability under the conditions and observation period tested. The supplied evidence does not report long-term safety or efficacy data, nor does it provide information on human trials or broader applicability to other HPV types.

阴道上皮内瘤变:HPV16和初始分级作为10年系列中复发的预测因子。

Vaginal Intraepithelial Neoplasia: HPV16 and Grade as Recurrence Predictors in a 10-Year Series.
作者： Ignacio Cristóbal Quevedo, Leonor González-Arroyo Ferrández, Belen Lopez-Cavanillas, Cristina González-Benítez, L. Yébenes-Gregorio, María Serrano Velasco, Belén López-Cavanillas, Cristina Gonzalez, Laura Yebenes Gregorio, Belén López Cavanillas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述在10年期间患有阴道上皮内瘤变(VaIN)的患者中,高危复发的临床特征、治疗模式及相关因素。
设计与对象
本研究是一项从2013年至2023年的回顾性单中心队列研究研究,包括71例经组织学确诊的VaIN患者。
核心方法
主要结局是经组织学确诊的高危复发或进展(HSIL+:VaIN2–3或浸润性癌),随访中出现的VaIN1不被视为复发。研究分析了HPV基因型及其与复发结局的关系。
主要结果
平均年龄为47.9±10.5岁。初始分级中,VaIN1/LSIL占59.2%,VaIN2占32.4%,VaIN3占8.5%。高危型HPV在71.8%的患者中被检测到,其中HPV16占22.5%,52.8%的患者存在多重感染。
解释与创新
研究发现,HPV16阳性患者的HSIL+-无复发生存期较短,尽管HSIL+率的粗略差异不具有统计学意义。初始HSIL显示出向更高复发率发展的非显著趋势。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,HPV16和VaIN的初始分级可能是预测高危复发的重要因素,这可能有助于制定更针对性的监测策略,以应对HPV相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于事件数量较少(12个)以及某些结果中的非显著趋势,这可能影响结论的强度。然而,这些产生假设的发现支持进一步的前瞻性评估。

Vaginal Intraepithelial Neoplasia: HPV16 and Grade as Recurrence Predictors in a 10-Year Series.

Authors: Ignacio Cristóbal Quevedo, Leonor González-Arroyo Ferrández, Belen Lopez-Cavanillas, Cristina González-Benítez, L. Yébenes-Gregorio, María Serrano Velasco, Belén López-Cavanillas, Cristina Gonzalez, Laura Yebenes Gregorio, Belén López Cavanillas
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives: To characterize the clinical profile, treatment patterns and factors associated with high-grade recurrence in patients with vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN) at a tertiary referral center over 10 years. Methods: Retrospective single-center cohort (2013–2023) of 71 consecutive patients with histologically confirmed VaIN. Primary outcome: histologically confirmed high-grade recurrence or progression (HSIL+: VaIN2–3 or invasive carcinoma); VaIN1 on follow-up was not considered recurrence. Results: Mean age 47.9±10.5 years. Index grade: VaIN1/LSIL 59.2%, VaIN2 32.4%, VaIN3 8.5%. High-risk HPV was detected in 71.8% and HPV16 in 22.5%; 52.8% of genotyped patients had multiple infections. Over a median follow-up of 48 months [IQR 36–84], 12 patients (16.9%) developed HSIL+ (VaIN2 n=10, VaIN3 n=1, carcinoma n=1); median time to event was 14.5 months [IQR 8.8–27.5]; 24-month HSIL+-free probability was 88%. HPV16-positive patients had shorter HSIL+-free survival (log-rank p=0.046), although the crude difference in HSIL+ rates was not significant (31.3% vs 12.7%; OR 3.12, 95% CI 0.83–11.69; p=0.125). Index HSIL showed a non-significant trend (27.6% vs 9.5%; OR 3.62, 95% CI 0.97–13.46; p=0.059). Conclusions: In this series, HPV16 was associated with earlier high-grade recurrence, and index HSIL showed a consistent but non-significant trend. These hypothesis-generating findings, limited by 12 events, support prospective evaluation of genotype- and grade-based surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the clinical profile, treatment patterns and factors associated with high-grade recurrence in patients with vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN) over a 10-year period.
Design and population
A retrospective single-center cohort study was conducted from 2013 to 2023, including 71 consecutive patients with histologically confirmed VaIN.
Core methods
The primary outcome was histologically confirmed high-grade recurrence or progression (HSIL+: VaIN2–3 or invasive carcinoma), with VaIN1 on follow-up not considered recurrence. The study analyzed HPV genotypes and their association with recurrence outcomes.
Main results
The mean age was 47.9±10.5 years. Index grades were VaIN1/LSIL in 59.2%, VaIN2 in 32.4%, and VaIN3 in 8.5%. High-risk HPV was detected in 71.8% of patients, with HPV16 identified in 22.5% and 52.8% having multiple infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that HPV16-positive patients had shorter HSIL+-free survival, although the crude difference in HSIL+ rates was not statistically significant. Index HSIL showed a non-significant trend towards higher recurrence.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that HPV16 and the initial grade of VaIN may be important factors in predicting high-grade recurrence, which could inform more targeted surveillance strategies for HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by a small number of events (12) and a non-significant trend in some results, which may affect the strength of the conclusions. However, the hypothesis-generating nature of the findings supports further prospective evaluation.

Journal: International Journal of Cancer  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-18  |  DOI: 10.1002/ijc.70838  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

巴西口腔及肛生殖器人乳头瘤病毒基因型与头颈癌的关联:一项全国性病例对照研究。

The Association of Oral and Anogenital Human Papillomavirus Genotypes With Head and Neck Cancers in Brazil: A Nationwide Case-Control Study.
作者： Carlos Pilz, Gustavo Eidt, Natália Luiza Kops, Augusto Bacelo Bidinotto, Paulo Rocha Dornelles, Pâmela Cristina Gaspar, Daniel Moreira Alves da Silva, Allan Mascarenhas Amaral Barroso, Manoela Domingues Martins, Fernando Neves Hugo
摘要中文翻译摘要 本多中心病例对照研究(2019–2023年)评估了巴西口腔及肛生殖器人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型与头颈癌的关联。纳入病例为18岁及以上新诊断、组织学确认的口腔、口咽、下咽或喉部鳞状细胞癌患者。对照组(无癌症个体)按地区、性别和年龄进行后验匹配。口腔、生殖器和肛门样本在中央实验室进行HPV基因型检测。采用条件回归模型估计关联。分析包括314名参与者(157名病例,157名对照),其中大部分为男性(79%),年龄在50–65岁之间(51%)。口腔HPV 患病率在病例和对照组中相似(14% vs. 12%),主要涉及高危型基因型(40种中有35种)。病例中HPV-16/18感染率更高(7.3% vs. 1.9%;p = 0.024),其中大多数HPV-16/18阳性癌症位于口咽部(11例中有10例)。生殖器HPV 患病率感染率为39%,无病例对照差异,而肛门HPV 患病率感染率为17%。病例中任何高危型肛门HPV感染的比例高于对照组(16% vs. 最小的基因型一致性在口腔和肛生殖器部位之间观察到。当前吸烟者作为病例的几率是对照组的十倍(调整后OR 10.0;95% CI 2.92–34.6)。口腔HPV-16/18感染与头颈癌独立相关(调整后OR 6.30;95% CI 1.52–26.1)。多部位HPV基因型检测揭示了特定部位的定植模式,生殖器感染在男女中最为普遍。经过多变量调整后,仅口腔HPV-16/18感染与头颈癌有显著关联,尤其是口咽癌。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨巴西口腔及肛生殖器人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型与头颈癌的关联,以了解HPV在这些癌症中的作用。
设计与对象
一项于2019年至2023年期间在全国范围内进行的病例对照研究,涉及314名参与者(157名病例和157名对照),其中大部分为50–65岁男性。
核心方法
在中央实验室对口腔、生殖器和肛门样本进行HPV基因型检测,并采用条件回归模型估计关联。
主要结果
口腔HPV 患病率在病例和对照组中相似(14% vs. 12%),病例中HPV-16/18感染率更高(7.3% vs. 生殖器HPV 患病率感染率为39%,无病例对照差异,而肛门HPV 患病率感染率为17%。
解释与创新
研究发现,口腔HPV-16/18感染与头颈癌独立相关,尤其是口咽癌,突显了其在该疾病中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,口腔HPV-16/18可能是头颈癌的重要风险因素,特别是口咽癌,这可能有助于制定针对性的筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究样本量相对较小,并依赖于自我报告数据,这可能限制其普遍适用性。然而,由于采用了匹配病例对照设计和中央实验室处理,证据强度较强。

The Association of Oral and Anogenital Human Papillomavirus Genotypes With Head and Neck Cancers in Brazil: A Nationwide Case-Control Study.

Authors: Carlos Pilz, Gustavo Eidt, Natália Luiza Kops, Augusto Bacelo Bidinotto, Paulo Rocha Dornelles, Pâmela Cristina Gaspar, Daniel Moreira Alves da Silva, Allan Mascarenhas Amaral Barroso, Manoela Domingues Martins, Fernando Neves Hugo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT This multicenter case–control study assessed (2019–2023) the association of oral and anogenital human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes with head and neck cancers in Brazil. Eligible cases were adults (≥ 18 years) with newly diagnosed, histologically confirmed squamous cell carcinoma of the oral cavity, oropharynx, hypopharynx, or larynx. Controls (cancer‐free individuals) were matched a posteriori by region, sex, and age. Oral, genital, and anal samples were collected and processed at a central laboratory for HPV genotyping. Associations were estimated using conditional regression. Analyses included 314 participants (157 cases, 157 controls), mostly males (79%) aged 50–65 years (51%). Oral HPV prevalence was similar between cases and controls (14% vs. 12%), predominantly involving high‐risk genotypes (35 out of 40); HPV‐16/18 was more prevalent among cases (7.3% vs. 1.9%; p = 0.024), with most HPV‐16/18–positive cancers located in the oropharynx (10 out of 11). Genital HPV prevalence was 39% with no case–control differences, while anal HPV prevalence was 17%. Cases were more frequently positive for any high‐risk anal HPV type than controls (16% vs. Minimal genotype concordance was observed between oral and anogenital sites. Current smokers had ten‐fold higher odds of being cases (adjustedOR 10.0; 95% CI 2.92–34.6). Oral HPV‐16/18 infection was independently associated with head and neck cancer (adjustedOR 6.30; 95% CI 1.52–26.1). Multisite HPV genotyping revealed site‐specific colonization patterns, with genital infections being most prevalent among both men and women. After multivariable adjustment, only oral HPV‐16/18 infection was strongly associated with head and neck cancer, especially oropharyngeal carcinoma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the association of oral and anogenital human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes with head and neck cancers in Brazil, addressing the need to understand the role of HPV in these cancers.
Design and population
A nationwide case-control study conducted between 2019 and 2023, involving 314 participants (157 cases and 157 controls), mostly males aged 50–65 years.
Core methods
Oral, genital, and anal samples were collected and processed at a central laboratory for HPV genotyping, and associations were estimated using conditional regression.
Main results
Oral HPV prevalence was similar between cases and controls (14% vs. 12%), with HPV-16/18 more prevalent among cases (7.3% vs. Genital HPV prevalence was 39% with no case–control differences, while anal HPV prevalence was 17%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that oral HPV-16/18 infection was independently associated with head and neck cancer, especially oropharyngeal carcinoma, highlighting its potential role in this disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that oral HPV-16/18 may be a significant risk factor for head and neck cancers, particularly oropharyngeal carcinoma, which could inform targeted screening and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a relatively small sample size and relied on self-reported data, which may limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong due to the use of a matched case-control design and central laboratory processing.

社交媒体对荷兰年轻人2023-2024年HPV疫苗补种活动的洞察:一项定性研究

Social Media Insights Into the Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Catch-Up Campaign for Young Adults in the Netherlands, 2023-2024: Qualitative Study
作者： Eline E.M.W. van der Werf, Judith I. van Heugten, Kirsten M.C. Bisschops, Claire Lovell, Tamara Sonia Boender, van der Werf EEMW
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV疫苗可以预防与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关的癌症,但其接种率仍不理想。随着年轻人越来越多地在线获取健康信息,了解他们对此次活动的在线问题、担忧和错误信息,有助于改进未来的HPV疫苗接种工作。
设计与对象
在荷兰,一项补种活动于2023-2024年邀请了18至27岁的人群,提供额外的免费接种机会,使24%的受邀人群接种了疫苗。
核心方法
在对潜在平台和数据来源进行调查后,我们于2023年1月1日至2024年6月1日期间,在Instagram、Facebook和X(原Twitter)上进行了回顾性在线社交媒体监听分析,使用了来自阿姆斯特丹公共卫生服务、荷兰国家公共卫生与环境研究所、荷兰公共广播基金会和LINDA.meiden的数据。手动收集了与HPV疫苗相关的公开帖子、评论和互动,并进行了归纳编码。
主要结果
A total of 821 posts and 5157 comments were collected and analyzed. Ten recurring themes were identified across varying risk levels. Two themes were classified as high risk: an information gap regarding HPV vaccination effectiveness and concerns about the transparency regarding HPV vaccine side effects. Three themes were classified as medium risk: questioning HPV vaccine effectiveness, reduced general vaccine trust following COVID-19, and accessibility issues related to vaccination locations and service times. Two themes were classified as neutral risk: questions regarding campaign eligibility criteria and calls for broader HPV prevention measures.
解释与创新
本研究试点了在线社交媒体监听流程,以识别年轻人对HPV疫苗补种活动的可操作性见解,包括相关问题、担忧和错误信息。此次试点表明,即使资源有限,也可以有效实施在线社交媒体监听。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过突出需要解决HPV疫苗有效性及副作用的错误信息和信息缺口,为未来的HPV疫苗接种工作提供信息。它们还表明了改善沟通和减少HPV疫苗接种实践障碍的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖手动收集和编码的社交媒体数据,这可能引入偏差。由于研究基于资源和范围有限的试点,其证据强度为中等。

Social Media Insights Into the Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Catch-Up Campaign for Young Adults in the Netherlands, 2023-2024: Qualitative Study

Authors: Eline E.M.W. van der Werf, Judith I. van Heugten, Kirsten M.C. Bisschops, Claire Lovell, Tamara Sonia Boender, van der Werf EEMW
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination prevents HPV-related cancers, but its uptake remains suboptimal. In the Netherlands, a catch-up campaign invited individuals aged 18 to 27 years for an additional cost-free opportunity to initiate or complete their HPV vaccination in 2023‐2024, which led to 24% of the invited population getting vaccinated. As young adults increasingly seek health information online, understanding online questions, concerns, and misinformation in response to the campaign may help improve future vaccination efforts. In addition to the overall evaluation of the vaccination campaign, we piloted online social listening processes to generate an insight report for the Public Health Service (PHS) Amsterdam. Objective This study piloted online social listening processes at PHS Amsterdam to identify actionable insights into questions, concerns, and misinformation surrounding the HPV vaccination catch-up campaign among young adults. Methods Following an inventory of potential platforms and data sources, a retrospective online social listening analysis was conducted across Instagram, Facebook, and X (formerly Twitter), using data from PHS Amsterdam, the National Institute for Public Health and the Environment, the Dutch public broadcasting foundation, and LINDA.meiden from January 1, 2023, to June 1, 2024. Publicly available posts, comments, and engagement concerning HPV vaccination were manually collected and inductively coded. Themes were assessed for risk based on expected potential harm and observed reach and were used to develop recommendations. Results A total of 821 posts and 5157 comments were collected and analyzed. Ten recurring themes were identified across varying risk levels. Two themes were classified as high risk: an information gap regarding HPV vaccination effectiveness and concerns about the transparency regarding HPV vaccine side effects. Three themes were classified as medium risk: questioning HPV vaccine effectiveness, reduced general vaccine trust following COVID-19, and accessibility issues related to vaccination locations and service times. Two themes were classified as neutral risk: questions regarding campaign eligibility criteria and calls for broader HPV prevention measures. Finally, 3 low-risk themes concerned questions regarding HPV vaccine brand specifics, campaign choices, and scheduling HPV appointments. Comments also referred to subsequent information sources, including reliable sources, false narratives, and misinformation. Recommendations focused on addressing questions and information gaps (ie, improving risk perception and providing more detailed HPV vaccine information), improving communication, and reducing practical barriers to vaccination. Conclusions Manual social listening across selected online sources provided actionable insights into the public discourse during the HPV vaccination catch-up campaign. Results mostly reflected questions, concerns, and information gaps. Although some themes were critical of HPV vaccination, false narratives and misinformation did not dominate the discourse. Recommendations aim to reduce practical barriers and address information needs. This pilot showed that online social listening can be effectively implemented even with limited resources.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination prevents HPV-related cancers, but its uptake remains suboptimal. As young adults increasingly seek health information online, understanding online questions, concerns, and misinformation in response to the campaign may help improve future vaccination efforts.
Design and population
In the Netherlands, a catch-up campaign invited individuals aged 18 to 27 years for an additional cost-free opportunity to initiate or complete their HPV vaccination in 2023‐2024, which led to 24% of the invited population getting vaccinated.
Core methods

Publicly available posts, comments, and engagement concerning HPV vaccination were manually collected and inductively coded.
Main results
A total of 821 posts and 5157 comments were collected and analyzed. Ten recurring themes were identified across varying risk levels. Two themes were classified as high risk: an information gap regarding HPV vaccination effectiveness and concerns about the transparency regarding HPV vaccine side effects. Three themes were classified as medium risk: questioning HPV vaccine effectiveness, reduced general vaccine trust following COVID-19, and accessibility issues related to vaccination locations and service times. Two themes were classified as neutral risk: questions regarding campaign eligibility criteria and calls for broader HPV prevention measures.
Interpretation and novelty
The study piloted online social listening processes to identify actionable insights into questions, concerns, and misinformation surrounding the HPV vaccination catch-up campaign among young adults. This pilot showed that online social listening can be effectively implemented even with limited resources.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future vaccination efforts by highlighting the need to address information gaps and misinformation about HPV vaccine effectiveness and side effects. They also suggest the importance of improving communication and reducing practical barriers to vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on manually collected and coded social media data, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on a pilot study with limited resources and scope.

老挝北部各省女性中患病率及人乳头瘤病毒基因型分布情况,2018-2019年。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus in women from northern provinces of Lao People's Democratic Republic, 2018-2019.
作者： Vilaysone Khounvisith, Bounthome Samountry, Janko van Beek, Aurélie Sausy, Regina Brunnhoefer, Bounta Vongphachanh, Khamtim Samountry, Maude Pauly, Joel Mossong, Jessica Tapp
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌是全球女性中导致发病率和死亡率的常见原因,并且与高危型(HR)人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)基因型的存在有强烈关联。本研究旨在确定Lao女性中HPV的患病率及其基因型,并探讨其与细胞学检查结果的关系。方法:2018年10月至2019年2月期间,邀请了来自Xiengkhouang、Luangprabang、Oudomxay和Huaphan省的1302名无症状女性(平均年龄37.4岁,年龄范围18-76岁)填写问卷,并采集宫颈细胞进行细胞学检查,以及采集拭子用于PCR和AnyplexTM II HPV-HR检测。结果:总体而言,1268名可评估样本的女性中有114人(9.0%)细胞学检查结果异常,3人(0.2%)患有宫颈癌。AnyplexTM II HPV-HR试剂盒在1184名参与者中检测出256例阳性(21.6%),其中最常见的高危型基因型为52型(20.4%)、58型(12.8%)和16型(11.7%)。多变量分析显示,年龄≥40岁的参与者(OR:1.6;95% CI:1.1-2.4,p = 0.015)以及居住在Luangprabang省的参与者(OR:1.9;95% CI:1.1-3.2,p = 0.02)比年轻女性和居住在Huaphan省的女性更可能有异常细胞学检查结果。单身、离婚或丧偶的女性比已婚女性更可能检测到HPV(OR:2.3;95% CI:1.5-3.7,p < 0.001)。结论:HPV 52、58和16型是最常见的高危型基因型,这清楚地表明应引入非十价疫苗。对于年龄太大而无法从最近引入的疫苗中受益的女性,应考虑定期筛查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌是全球女性中常见的疾病原因和死亡率,并且与高危型(HR)人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型的存在有强烈关联。

核心方法
AnyplexTM II HPV-HR试剂盒在1184名参与者中检测出256例阳性(21.6%),其中最常见的高危型基因型为52型(20.4%)、58型(12.8%)和16型(11.7%)。
主要结果
总体而言,在1268名具有可评估样本的女性中,有114人(9.0%)细胞学结果异常,3人(0.2%)患有宫颈癌。
解释与创新
多变量分析显示,年龄≥40岁的参与者(OR:1.6;95% CI:1.1-2.4,p = 0.015)以及居住在Luangprabang省的参与者(OR:1.9;95% CI:1.1-3.2,p = 0.02)比年轻女性和居住在Huaphan省的女性更可能有异常细胞学结果。

单身、离婚或丧偶的女性比已婚女性更可能检测到HPV(OR:2.3;95% CI:1.5-3.7,p < 0.001)。

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus in women from northern provinces of Lao People's Democratic Republic, 2018-2019.

Authors: Vilaysone Khounvisith, Bounthome Samountry, Janko van Beek, Aurélie Sausy, Regina Brunnhoefer, Bounta Vongphachanh, Khamtim Samountry, Maude Pauly, Joel Mossong, Jessica Tapp
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer is a frequent cause of morbidity and mortality in women worldwide and strongly linked to the presence of high-risk (HR) human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes. The present study aimed to determine HPV prevalence and genotypes and their relationship with cytology results in Lao women. METHODS: Between October 2018 and February 2019, a total of 1302 asymptomatic women (mean age 37.4 years, range 18-76) from Xiengkhouang, Luangprabang, Oudomxay and Huaphan provinces were invited to answer a questionnaire, and cervical cells were collected for cytology and a swab for PCR and AnyplexTM II HPV-HR detection. RESULTS: Overall, 114 of 1268 women with evaluable samples (9.0%) had abnormal cytology and three (0.2%) had cervical cancer. AnyplexTM II HPV-HR kit detected 256 positives among 1184 participants (21.6%), with types 52, 58 and 16 being the most prevalent HR genotypes (20.4%, 12.8% and 11.7%, respectively). Multivariable analysis showed that participants aged ≥ 40 years (OR: 1.6; 95% CI: 1.1-2.4, p = 0.015) and those living in Luangprabang province (OR: 1.9; 95% CI: 1.1-3.2, p = 0.02) had a higher likelihood of abnormal cytology compared to younger women and those residing in Huaphan. Single/divorced/widow women had a higher probability of HPV detection compared to married women (OR: 2.3; 95% CI: 1.5-3.7, p < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: HPV 52, 58 and 16 were the most prevalent high-risk genotypes, clearly advocating for the introduction of the nonavalent vaccine. Periodic screening of women too old to benefit from the recently introduced vaccine should be considered.

Question and background
Cervical cancer is a frequent cause of morbidity and mortality in women worldwide and strongly linked to the presence of high-risk (HR) human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes.

Core methods
AnyplexTM II HPV-HR kit detected 256 positives among 1184 participants (21.6%), with types 52, 58 and 16 being the most prevalent HR genotypes (20.4%, 12.8% and 11.7%, respectively).
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
Multivariable analysis showed that participants aged ≥ 40 years (OR: 1.6; 95% CI: 1.1-2.4, p = 0.015) and those living in Luangprabang province (OR: 1.9; 95% CI: 1.1-3.2, p = 0.02) had a higher likelihood of abnormal cytology compared to younger women and those residing in Huaphan.

宫颈癌风险降低在人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种时代的影响。

Cervical cancer risk reduction in the era of human papillomavirus vaccination.
作者： Sarah A. Phillips, Priti Bandi, Jessica Star, Sarah M. Temkin, Robert A. Smith, Rebecca Landy
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 在评估宫颈筛查参与对宫颈癌风险的群体影响时,需要考虑HPV(人乳头瘤病毒)疫苗接种状态,但筛查率很少按疫苗接种状态进行报告,限制了对群体层面宫颈癌保护效果的评估。方法 使用2019年国家健康访谈调查中21–39岁女性的数据(N = 4000),作者报告了按年龄和自述HPV 疫苗接种状态分层的宫颈筛查参与比例。通过假设HPV 疫苗接种和及时筛查状态分别将宫颈癌风险降低85%和70%,作者探讨了不同筛查覆盖率下宫颈癌风险的群体层面降低情况。结果 总共有78.4%的女性及时接受了筛查,38.0%的女性报告曾接受过HPV 疫苗,15.3%的女性既未接种疫苗也未及时接受筛查。在不同年龄组中,接种疫苗的女性及时接受筛查的比例比未接种疫苗的女性高出6.3%–19.1%。接种HPV 疫苗接种和及时筛查对宫颈癌风险的相对降低范围从36–39岁女性的62.1%到26–30岁女性的73.1%,与未接种HPV 疫苗接种且未及时筛查的情况相比。尽管21–25岁女性的筛查参与率最低,但由于其HPV 疫苗接种水平较高,其宫颈癌风险降低幅度并非最低。结论 接种过HPV疫苗的女性宫颈筛查率更高,导致宫颈癌风险的群体层面降低幅度高于仅考虑HPV 疫苗接种和筛查率独立情况的预期值。将HPV 疫苗接种状态纳入国家监测数据集有助于识别和针对高风险社区进行干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估宫颈筛查参与对宫颈癌风险的群体影响,特别是在与HPV 疫苗接种状态相关的情况下,因为筛查率很少按疫苗接种状态进行报告,限制了对群体层面宫颈癌保护效果的评估。
设计与对象
本研究使用了2019年国家健康访谈调查的数据,包括4000名21–39岁的女性,以报告按年龄和自述HPV 疫苗接种状态分层的宫颈筛查参与比例。
核心方法
作者分析了2019年国家健康访谈调查的数据,以报告按年龄和自述HPV 疫苗接种状态分层的宫颈筛查参与比例。他们假设HPV 疫苗接种和及时筛查状态分别将宫颈癌风险降低85%和70%,以探讨不同筛查覆盖率下宫颈癌风险的群体层面降低情况。
主要结果
结果显示,78.4%的女性及时接受了筛查,38.0%的女性报告曾接受过HPV 疫苗,15.3%的女性既未接种疫苗也未及时接受筛查。在不同年龄组中,接种疫苗的女性及时接受筛查的比例比未接种疫苗的女性高出6.3%–19.1%。
解释与创新
作者认为,接种过HPV疫苗的女性宫颈筛查率更高,导致宫颈癌风险的群体层面降低幅度高于仅考虑HPV 疫苗接种和筛查率独立情况的预期值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,将HPV 疫苗接种状态纳入国家监测数据集有助于识别和针对高风险社区进行干预,从而改善宫颈癌的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于依赖自述数据,未考虑潜在的偏倚或混杂因素。证据强度中等,因为它是基于调查数据而非直接的临床或生物学测量。

Cervical cancer risk reduction in the era of human papillomavirus vaccination.

Authors: Sarah A. Phillips, Priti Bandi, Jessica Star, Sarah M. Temkin, Robert A. Smith, Rebecca Landy
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination status is important to consider when evaluating the population‐level impact of cervical screening uptake on cervical cancer risk, yet screening rates are rarely presented by vaccination status, limiting assessments of population‐level protection against cervical cancer. Methods Using females 21–39 years old from the 2019 National Health Interview Survey ( N = 4000), the authors report the proportion up‐to‐date with cervical screening stratified by age and self‐reported HPV vaccination status. Using assumptions of 85% and 70% reduction in cervical cancer risk from HPV vaccination and up‐to‐date screening status, respectively, the authors explore the population‐level reduction in cervical cancer risk from varying screening coverage by HPV vaccination status. Results A total of 78.4% of females were up‐to‐date with screening, 38.0% reported ever receiving an HPV vaccine, and 15.3% were both unvaccinated and not up‐to‐date with screening. Across age groups, the proportion of females up‐to‐date with screening was 6.3%–19.1% higher for vaccinated versus unvaccinated females. The relative reduction in cervical cancer risk from vaccination and screening ranged from 62.1% for females 36–39 years old to 73.1% for females 26–30 years old, compared to the absence of vaccination and screening. Despite females 21–25 years old having the lowest screening uptake, their reduction in cervical cancer risk was not the lowest due to higher vaccination levels. Conclusions Cervical screening rates are higher among females who were vaccinated against HPV, resulting in a higher population‐level risk of cervical cancer than expected from considering vaccination and screening rates independently. Including vaccination status in national surveillance data sets would help identify and target interventions within communities at higher risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the population-level impact of cervical screening uptake on cervical cancer risk in relation to HPV vaccination status, as screening rates are rarely presented by vaccination status, limiting assessments of population-level protection against cervical cancer.
Design and population
The study used data from the 2019 National Health Interview Survey, including 4000 females aged 21–39 years, to report the proportion up-to-date with cervical screening stratified by age and self-reported HPV vaccination status.
Core methods
The authors analyzed data from the 2019 National Health Interview Survey to report the proportion of females up-to-date with cervical screening, stratified by age and self-reported HPV vaccination status. They used assumptions of 85% and 70% reduction in cervical cancer risk from HPV vaccination and up-to-date screening status, respectively, to explore population-level risk reduction.
Main results
Results showed that 78.4% of females were up-to-date with screening, 38.0% reported ever receiving an HPV vaccine, and 15.3% were both unvaccinated and not up-to-date with screening. Across age groups, the proportion of females up-to-date with screening was 6.3%–19.1% higher for vaccinated versus unvaccinated females.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that cervical screening rates are higher among females who were vaccinated against HPV, resulting in a higher population-level risk of cervical cancer than expected from considering vaccination and screening rates independently.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating vaccination status into national surveillance data sets could help identify and target interventions within communities at higher risk, thereby improving cervical cancer prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and does not account for potential biases or confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on survey data rather than direct clinical or biological measurements.

人乳头瘤病毒感染和疫苗接种在波兰医学学生中的知识空白:一项基于项目的横断面分析,嵌入式多学科教育网络研讨会的可行性评估

Knowledge gaps in human papillomavirus infection and vaccination among Polish medical students: an item-level cross-sectional analysis with an embedded feasibility evaluation of a multidisciplinary educational webinar
作者： Łukasz Duda-Duma, Ernest Kuchar, Arkadiusz Kozioł, Maria Gańczak, Maria Ganczak, Duda-Duma Ł
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
波兰于2023年6月才引入针对青少年的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种,覆盖率仍然很低。由于临床医生的建议对接受率有重大影响,未来医生的准备情况至关重要。
设计与对象
研究是在2024年2月在波兰Zielona Góra大学进行的一项横断面知识评估,嵌入了单臂预-后可行性组件。所有96名第四年医学学生,均未开始传染病课程,被邀请参加一个自愿的五小时专家指导网络研讨会。
核心方法
在研讨会前和后立即进行了一个包含20个单选题的问卷调查。主要结果是项目难度;分析包括Wilson 95%置信区间、修正后的项目-总相关性、Kuder-Richardson公式20(KR-20)及其删除项目后的修正值,以及McNemar精确检验,使用Bonferroni-Holm校正。
主要结果
96名受邀学生中有54人(56.2%)提供了完整的配对数据:37名女性,17名男性。基线得分的平均值为13.19/20(标准差3.76);KR-20值为0.79。各项目的表现差异超过五倍(18.5–98.1%),因此平均值掩盖了教育相关的模式。有四个项目被宫颈癌或更少的学生正确回答:宫颈癌作为感染的罕见个体结果(18.5%),宫颈癌在乌克兰的负担(20.4%),疫苗的注册年龄(35.2%)和成人计划(50.0%)。所有54名学生随后在即时非监考的后续测试中均获得20/20的满分,但这一天花板效应无法解释为学习效果。
解释与创新
知识缺陷集中在HPV预防的应用和流行病学内容上,包括适用性、年龄指示、剂量计划和感染后的个体癌症风险,而不是病毒生物学或其疫苗。该模块的交付和评估是可行的;需要进行有监考的延迟评估的控制研究,以证明其有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要针对HPV预防的知识空白进行有针对性的教育干预,特别是关于流行病学和应用方面。它们还强调了在实施前评估教育模块的有效性的重要性。
局限与证据强度
研究受到横断面设计的限制,缺乏监考的延迟评估来确定学习效果。证据为中等,提供了关于知识空白的见解,但未建立因果关系或长期影响。

Knowledge gaps in human papillomavirus infection and vaccination among Polish medical students: an item-level cross-sectional analysis with an embedded feasibility evaluation of a multidisciplinary educational webinar

Authors: Łukasz Duda-Duma, Ernest Kuchar, Arkadiusz Kozioł, Maria Gańczak, Maria Ganczak, Duda-Duma Ł
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Poland introduced universal adolescent human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination only in June 2023, and coverage remains low. Because clinicians' recommendations strongly influence uptake, future physicians' preparedness matters. Polish studies report aggregate scores, which cannot show which facts are missing and so cannot guide curricular change. We mapped fourth-year medical students' baseline HPV knowledge at item level and assessed the feasibility of a multidisciplinary educational module. Methods In February 2024, within the Horizon 2020 project RIVER-EU, we conducted a cross-sectional knowledge assessment with an embedded single-arm pre–post feasibility component at the University of Zielona Góra, Poland. All 96 fourth-year students, none of whom had begun the infectious-diseases course, were invited to a voluntary five-hour expert-led webinar. A 20-item single-choice questionnaire was administered immediately before and after. The primary outcome was item difficulty; analyses comprised Wilson 95% confidence intervals, corrected item–total correlations, Kuder–Richardson Formula 20 (KR-20) with KR-20-if-item-deleted, and McNemar's exact test with Bonferroni–Holm correction. Reporting follows TREND. Results Fifty-four of 96 invited students (56.2%) provided complete paired data: 37 women, 17 men. Mean baseline score was 13.19/20 (SD 3.76); KR-20 0.79. Performance varied more than fivefold across items (18.5–98.1%), so the mean concealed the educationally relevant pattern. Four items were answered correctly by half the cohort or fewer: cervical cancer as an uncommon individual outcome of infection (18.5%), the cervical cancer burden in Ukraine (20.4%), the vaccines' registered age (35.2%) and adult schedules (50.0%). All four discriminated acceptably (corrected item–total r 0.23–0.44), so none is an artifact of item construction. Natural history and epidemiology was the weakest domain (50.0%), virology and vaccine science the strongest (77.0%); sexes did not differ (p = 0.27). All 54 subsequently scored 20/20, but this ceiling, on an immediate unproctored post-test, is uninterpretable as learning. Conclusions Deficits concentrated in the applied and epidemiological content of HPV prevention - eligibility, age indications, dose schedules and individual cancer risk after infection not in the biology of the virus or its vaccines. The module was feasible to deliver and evaluate; controlled studies with proctored, delayed assessment are needed before claiming effectiveness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Poland introduced universal adolescent human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination only in June 2023, and coverage remains low. Because clinicians' recommendations strongly influence uptake, future physicians' preparedness matters.
Design and population
The study was a cross-sectional knowledge assessment with an embedded single-arm pre–post feasibility component conducted at the University of Zielona Góra, Poland, in February 2024. All 96 fourth-year medical students, none of whom had begun the infectious-diseases course, were invited to a voluntary five-hour expert-led webinar.
Core methods
A 20-item single-choice questionnaire was administered immediately before and after the webinar. The primary outcome was item difficulty; analyses comprised Wilson 95% confidence intervals, corrected item–total correlations, Kuder–Richardson Formula 20 (KR-20) with KR-20-if-item-deleted, and McNemar's exact test with Bonferroni–Holm correction.
Main results
Fifty-four of 96 invited students (56.2%) provided complete paired data: 37 women, 17 men. The mean baseline score was 13.19/20 (SD 3.76); KR-20 0.79. Performance varied more than fivefold across items (18.5–98.1%), so the mean concealed the educationally relevant pattern. Four items were answered correctly by half the cohort or fewer: cervical cancer as an uncommon individual outcome of infection (18.5%), the cervical cancer burden in Ukraine (20.4%), the vaccines' registered age (35.2%) and adult schedules (50.0%). All 54 subsequently scored 20/20, but this ceiling, on an immediate unproctored post-test, is uninterpretable as learning.
Interpretation and novelty
Deficits concentrated in the applied and epidemiological content of HPV prevention - eligibility, age indications, dose schedules and individual cancer risk after infection not in the biology of the virus or its vaccines. The module was feasible to deliver and evaluate; controlled studies with proctored, delayed assessment are needed before claiming effectiveness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted educational interventions to address knowledge gaps in HPV prevention, particularly regarding epidemiological and applied aspects. They also highlight the importance of evaluating educational modules for effectiveness before implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its cross-sectional design and the lack of proctored, delayed assessment to determine learning effectiveness. The evidence is moderate as it provides insights into knowledge gaps but does not establish causality or long-term impact.

人乳头瘤病毒检测与宫颈细胞学检查在多米尼加共和国HIV阳性女性中对宫颈癌筛查的比较。

Human Papillomavirus Testing Compared With Cytology for Cervical Cancer Screening Among Women Living With HIV in the Dominican Republic.
作者： Margaret M Madeleine, Flavia Lantigua, Arismendy Benítez Abreu, Hoisex Gomez, Estefani Sánchez Marte, María Isabel Perez, Felix de Jesus Padilla Torres, Ernestina Hernández, Angélica Mondragón, Kelly Shrader
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HIV阳性女性(WWH)面临更高的宫颈癌风险,但在资源匮乏地区支持以人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)作为主要筛查手段的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们比较了在多米尼加共和国的WWH中HPV检测与细胞学检查在宫颈癌前病变检测中的表现,以制定国家筛查策略。
核心方法
本研究来自美国-拉丁美洲-加勒比海HIV/HPV-癌症预防临床试验网络第302项试验(Estudio Oportunidad,ClinicalTrials.gov编号:NCT05556772),共有575名WWH提供了由医护人员采集和自我采集的样本用于细胞学检查和HPV分层检测。
主要结果
任何筛查检测结果为阳性的女性,其HIV病毒载量更高,CD4细胞计数更低,且年龄更小。
解释与创新
Sensitivity to detect cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3+) by cytology (66.7% [95% CI, 49.2 to 80.5]) was lower than sensitivity of provider-collected results with either 13 high-risk HPV (hrHPV) genotypes (93.9 [95% CI, 78.7 to 98.5]) or 8 hrHPV genotypes (84.8% [95% CI, 68.3 to 98.6]).
科研与公卫意义
在多米尼加共和国的HIV阳性女性中,无论是医护人员采集还是自我采集的HPV检测,均在检测CIN3+方面优于细胞学检查。
局限与证据强度
这些发现支持在多米尼加共和国将主要筛查策略从细胞学检查转向基于HPV的检测,以提高癌前病变的检测准确性并减少WWH中的宫颈癌负担。

Human Papillomavirus Testing Compared With Cytology for Cervical Cancer Screening Among Women Living With HIV in the Dominican Republic.

Authors: Margaret M Madeleine, Flavia Lantigua, Arismendy Benítez Abreu, Hoisex Gomez, Estefani Sánchez Marte, María Isabel Perez, Felix de Jesus Padilla Torres, Ernestina Hernández, Angélica Mondragón, Kelly Shrader
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Women living with HIV (WWH) face elevated cervical cancer risk, yet evidence supporting primary human papillomavirus (HPV) testing in low-resource settings remains limited. We compared HPV testing with cytology for detection of cervical precancer among WWH in the Dominican Republic to inform national screening strategies. METHODS: In this cross-sectional analysis from US-Latin American-Caribbean HIV/HPV-Cancer Prevention Clinical Trials Network Trial 302 (Estudio Oportunidad, ClinicalTrials.gov identifier: NCT05556772), 575 WWH contributed provider- and self-collected samples for cytology and HPV hierarchical testing. Women with positive results on cytology, provider-collected HPV testing, or self-collected HPV testing were referred for colposcopy and biopsy. Screening test characteristics were evaluated using centrally adjudicated histologic end points. RESULTS: Women with positive results on any screening test had higher HIV viral loads, lower CD4 counts, and were younger than women with screen-negative results. Sensitivity to detect cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3+) by cytology (66.7% [95% CI, 49.2 to 80.5]) was lower than sensitivity of provider-collected results with either 13 high-risk HPV (hrHPV) genotypes (93.9 [95% CI, 78.7 to 98.5]) or 8 hrHPV genotypes (84.8% [95% CI, 68.3 to 98.6]). Self-collected samples had the highest sensitivity (97.0%, 95% CI, 81.3 to 99.6). Cytology missed 33.3% of CIN3+, hrHPV testing of provider-collected samples missed 6.1%-15.2% of CIN3+, and hrHPV testing of self-collected samples missed 3.0% of CIN3+. CONCLUSION: Among WWH in the Dominican Republic, HPV testing, whether provider- or self-collected, outperformed cytology for detection of CIN3+. Both 13- and 8-genotype hrHPV panels demonstrated high sensitivity but low specificity, resulting in high referral rates to colposcopy. These findings support transitioning to primary HPV-based screening for WWH in the Dominican Republic to increase accuracy in detecting precancer and reduce the cervical cancer burden among WWH.

Question and background

Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Women with positive results on any screening test had higher HIV viral loads, lower CD4 counts, and were younger than women with screen-negative results.
Interpretation and novelty
Sensitivity to detect cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3+) by cytology (66.7% [95% CI, 49.2 to 80.5]) was lower than sensitivity of provider-collected results with either 13 high-risk HPV (hrHPV) genotypes (93.9 [95% CI, 78.7 to 98.5]) or 8 hrHPV genotypes (84.8% [95% CI, 68.3 to 98.6]).

骨髓间充质干细胞通过激活miR-129诱导HPV相关宫颈癌上皮间质转化的分子机制研究。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.
作者： Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 miR-129 在 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 中的作用,解决 miR-129 与肿瘤进展之间尚不清楚的关系。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 77 个 宫颈癌 和 30 个正常宫颈组织样本,其中 BMSC 与 SiHa 和 HeLa 细胞共培养。
核心方法
通过 qRT-PCR 测量 miR-129 表达,并分析与临床病理特征和 OS 的关联。将BMSCs与SiHa和HeLa细胞共培养,检测细胞增殖、侵袭以及E-cadherin、N-cadherin和MRP1的表达。
主要结果
与正常对照相比,宫颈癌 组织中 miR-129 显着上调。miR-129 高表达与肿瘤大小、FIGO 分期和淋巴结转移相关,并且与较短的 OS 相关。BMSC 共培养上调 miR-129,促进增殖、集落形成和侵袭,而 miR-129 敲低则抑制这些表型。
解释与创新
该研究表明 miR-129 可能有助于 宫颈癌 的进展,但还需要进一步的体内和机制研究。
科研与公卫意义
miR-129 显示出作为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的诊断和预后生物标志物的潜力,并且 BMSCs/miR-129 轴可能有助于 宫颈癌 的进展。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外模型的使用和进一步体内验证的需要的限制。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.

Authors: Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide. The role of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: To investigate miR-129 expression and its clinical significance in HPV-associated cervical cancer and to explore whether BMSCs regulate malignant phenotypes through miR-129. METHODS: miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR in 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissues. Associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival (OS) were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve. Rat BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells. Following miR-129 overexpression or knockdown, cell proliferation (CCK-8, colony formation), invasion (Transwell) and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin and MRP1 (Western blot) were examined. RESULTS: miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034) and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression (SiHa: 3.52±0.42 vs. miR-129 overexpression promoted proliferation, colony formation and invasion, while knockdown suppressed these phenotypes. Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs. 1.00±0.08, P<0.01) and increased N-cadherin (2.85±0.22 vs. 1.00±0.10, P<0.01) and MRP1 (2.46±0.18 vs. Knockdown of miR-129 partially reversed BMSC-induced changes in these proteins. CONCLUSION: miR-129 is significantly upregulated in HPV-associated cervical cancer and shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker. BMSCs upregulate miR-129 in cervical cancer cells and miR-129 modulates proliferation, invasion, EMT marker expression and MRP1 expression. These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of miR-129 in HPV-associated cervical cancer, addressing the unclear relationship between miR-129 and tumor progression.
Design and population
The study involved 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissue samples, with BMSCs co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells.
Core methods
miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR, and associations with clinicopathological features and OS were analyzed. BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells, and cell proliferation, invasion, and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin, and MRP1 were examined.
Main results
miR-129 was significantly upregulated in cervical cancer tissues compared to normal controls. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size, FIGO stage, and lymph node metastasis, and was associated with shorter OS. BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129, promoting proliferation, colony formation, and invasion, while miR-129 knockdown suppressed these phenotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that miR-129 may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.
Scientific and public-health significance
miR-129 shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker for HPV-associated cervical cancer, and the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of in vitro models and the need for further in-vivo validation.

人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染及宫颈癌进展的神经免疫特征

The Neuroimmune Signature Underlying HPV Infection and Cervical Cancer Progression
作者： Yohan Lucas Gonçalves Corrêa, Júlia Nakanishi Usuda, Roseane Galdioli Nava, Fernando Yuri Nery do Vale, Debora Albuquerque, Anny Silva Adri, Ronaldy Santana Santos, Amanda Rodriguez Valera, Cabral Gustavo, H D Ochs
摘要中文翻译高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的持续感染是宫颈癌的主要病因,但伴随疾病进展的多细胞信号程序仍不完全明确。在这里,我们整合了单细胞RNA测序、独立的微阵列和批量RNA测序的元分析以及TCGA CESC肿瘤数据,以表征与HPV相关的宫颈疾病中的神经免疫转录架构。单细胞分析揭示了在HPV感染、高级别鳞状上皮内病变和宫颈癌过程中,上皮、基质、内皮和免疫组分的逐步重塑。尽管免疫失调占主导地位,但病变和肿瘤状态显示出与神经系统相关的程序的协调富集,包括突触组织、跨突触信号、轴突发育、神经发生和神经递质相关信号。这些程序与细胞因子产生、白细胞激活、趋化、细胞粘附、凋亡和免疫效应调节形成了相互连接的网络。跨平台整合识别出一个与肿瘤等级相关的12基因神经免疫特征,包括CXCL12、P2RY6、P2RX5和TACR1。配体受体推断进一步突出了谷氨酸能、肾上腺素能、去甲肾上腺素能、GABA能和嘌呤能信号通路。值得注意的是,这些信号分布在免疫、上皮、基质和内皮细胞群体中,而不是局限于经典的神经细胞组分。P2RX5与高级别肿瘤等级表现出最强的关联,而P2RY6、P2RY11和GABRP则表现出细胞类型和阶段依赖性的调控。综上所述,我们的研究结果定义了宫颈癌发生为一种分布式的神经免疫状态,在这种状态下,与HPV相关的免疫逃逸与异常神经递质传递相关程序汇聚,揭示了宫颈肿瘤微环境中潜在的生物标志物和可操作的信号轴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的持续感染是宫颈癌的主要病因,但伴随疾病进展的多细胞信号程序仍不完全明确。
设计与对象
在这里,我们整合了单细胞RNA测序、独立的微阵列和批量RNA测序的元分析以及TCGA CESC肿瘤数据,以表征与HPV相关的宫颈疾病中的神经免疫转录架构。
核心方法
单细胞分析揭示了在HPV感染、高级别鳞状上皮内病变和宫颈癌过程中,上皮、基质、内皮和免疫组分的逐步重塑。
主要结果
尽管免疫失调占主导地位,但病变和肿瘤状态显示出与神经系统相关的程序的协调富集,包括突触组织、跨突触信号、轴突发育、神经发生和神经递质相关信号。
解释与创新
这些程序与细胞因子产生、白细胞激活、趋化、细胞粘附、凋亡和免疫效应调节形成了相互连接的网络。
科研与公卫意义
跨平台整合识别出一个与肿瘤等级相关的12基因神经免疫特征,包括CXCL12、P2RY6、P2RX5和TACR1。
局限与证据强度
配体受体推断进一步突出了谷氨酸能、肾上腺素能、去甲肾上腺素能、GABA能和嘌呤能信号通路。

The Neuroimmune Signature Underlying HPV Infection and Cervical Cancer Progression

Authors: Yohan Lucas Gonçalves Corrêa, Júlia Nakanishi Usuda, Roseane Galdioli Nava, Fernando Yuri Nery do Vale, Debora Albuquerque, Anny Silva Adri, Ronaldy Santana Santos, Amanda Rodriguez Valera, Cabral Gustavo, H D Ochs
Original abstract (English)
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) is the principal cause of cervical cancer, yet the multicellular signalling programmes that accompany disease progression remain incompletely defined. Here, we integrated Single cell RNA sequencing, independent microarray and bulk RNA-sequencing meta analyses, and TCGA CESC tumor data to characterize the neuroimmune transcriptional architecture of HPV associated cervical disease. Single cell profiling revealed progressive remodeling of epithelial, stromal, endothelial and immune compartments across HPV infection, high grade squamous intraepithelial lesions and cervical cancer. Although immune dysregulation predominated, lesion and tumor states showed coordinated enrichment of nervous system associated programs, including synapse organization, trans-synaptic signaling, axon development, neurogenesis and neurotransmitter-related signaling. These programs formed interconnected networks with cytokine production, leukocyte activation, chemotaxis, cell adhesion, apoptosis, and immune effector regulation. Cross-platform integration identified a recurrent 12-gene neuroimmune signature associated with tumor grade related transcriptional variation, including CXCL12, P2RY6, P2RX5 and TACR1. Ligand receptor inference further highlighted glutamatergic, adrenergic, noradrenergic, GABAergic and purinergic signaling circuits. Notably, these signals were distributed across immune, epithelial, stromal and endothelial populations rather than confined to a canonical neural cell compartment. P2RX5 showed the strongest association with advanced tumor grade, whereas P2RY6, P2RY11 and GABRP displayed cell type and stage dependent regulation. Together, our findings define cervical carcinogenesis as a distributed neuroimmune state in which HPV associated immune evasion converges with ectopic neurotransmission-related programs, revealing candidate biomarkers and potentially actionable signaling axes in the cervical tumor microenvironment.

Design and population

Main results

Interpretation and novelty
These programs formed interconnected networks with cytokine production, leukocyte activation, chemotaxis, cell adhesion, apoptosis, and immune effector regulation.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

社区参与的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 自抽样用于性和性别少数人群 宫颈癌 筛查的可行性研究

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People
作者： Mollie Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shuying Sha
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:我们调查了肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。方法:我们利用虚拟访谈进行了一项描述性定性研究,以调查影响 CCS 的社会生态因素以及家庭 hrHPV 自我采样用于原发性 CCS 的可行性和可接受性。使用标准抽样和滚雪球抽样。使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。结果:19 名参与者在 2022-2023 年完成了 hrHPV 自我采样和定性访谈。确定的 6 个首要主题是 宫颈癌 筛查认知、既往负面医疗经历、hrHPV 自我采样的可取性、完成 hrHPV 自我采样、对 hrHPV 自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。结论:家庭 hrHPV 自我采样是一种有前景的干预措施,可增加 SGM 人群的 CCS。应让个人放心,自我取样是主要 CCS 的有效方法,并且他们很可能能够收集产生准确结果的样本。为了最大限度地提高 SGM 个人完成 CCS 的可能性,医疗保健提供者和医疗保健环境必须在整个 CCS 过程中成为肯定的、具有文化能力和知情的合作伙伴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。
设计与对象
我们在 2022 年至 2023 年期间对 19 名参与者进行了虚拟访谈,进行了一项描述性定性研究,以探讨影响 宫颈癌 筛查的社会生态因素以及在 SGM 个体中进行 hrHPV 自我采样的可行性。
核心方法
使用标准和滚雪球抽样来招募参与者,并使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。
主要结果
十九名参与者完成了高危型HPV自我采样和定性访谈,并识别出六个主要主题,涉及对宫颈癌筛查的看法、先前负面的医疗经历、高危型HPV自我采样的吸引力、完成高危型HPV自我采样的过程、对高危型HPV自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明,在家进行高危型HPV自我采样是一种有前景的干预措施,可提高SGM人群的宫颈癌筛查率,强调医疗提供者需要成为肯定和支持且具有文化能力的合作伙伴。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,高危型HPV自我采样可能是提高SGM个体宫颈癌筛查率的宝贵工具,可能有助于实现更公平的医疗保健获取和结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到定性设计和小样本量的限制,但鉴于其彻底的主题分析和团队成员共识,证据是强有力的。

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People

Authors: Mollie Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shuying Sha
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: We investigated the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians. Methods: We conducted a descriptive qualitative study using virtual interviews to investigate socioecological factors influencing CCS and the feasibility and acceptability of at-home hrHPV self-sampling for primary CCS. Criterion and snowball sampling were used. Data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach and team member consensus to identify salient themes. Results: Nineteen participants completed hrHPV self-sampling and qualitative interviews in 2022–2023. The 6 overarching themes identified were cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation. Conclusion: At-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase CCS in SGM people. Individuals should be reassured that self-sampling is an effective method for primary CCS and that they are likely to be able to collect a sample that will yield accurate results. To maximize the possibility of CCS completion by SGM individuals, healthcare providers and healthcare environments must be affirming, culturally competent, and informed partners throughout the CCS process.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians.
Design and population
A descriptive qualitative study was conducted using virtual interviews with 19 participants in 2022–2023 to explore socioecological factors influencing cervical cancer screening and the feasibility of hrHPV self-sampling in SGM individuals.
Core methods
Criterion and snowball sampling were used to recruit participants, and data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach with team member consensus to identify salient themes.
Main results
Nineteen participants completed hrHPV self-sampling and qualitative interviews, and six overarching themes were identified related to cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that at-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase cervical cancer screening in SGM people, highlighting the need for healthcare providers to be affirming and culturally competent partners.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that hrHPV self-sampling could be a valuable tool for improving cervical cancer screening rates among SGM individuals, potentially contributing to more equitable healthcare access and outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative design and small sample size, but the evidence is strong given the thorough thematic analysis and team member consensus.

克罗恩病长期免疫抑制治疗中出现多种肠外恶性肿瘤及与HPV16相关的肿瘤:一个病例报告案例

Multiple Extraintestinal Malignancies and HPV16-Associated Neoplasms in Crohn’s Disease Under Prolonged Immunosuppression: A Case Report
作者： Neuzenir da Silva de Abreu Oliveira, Karina França, Anna Karoline Fausto da Silva, Bernardo Acacio Santini Pereira, Ana Luiza Miguez Rodrigues, Juliana Werneck Coelho, Carlos Roberto Alves, Geovane Dias-Lopes, Susana Cristina Aide Viviani Fialho
摘要中文翻译背景:克罗恩病(CD)是一种胃肠道慢性炎症性疾病,其恶性肿瘤风险增加,尤其是在接受免疫抑制治疗的患者中。病例报告:本文描述了一位20岁女性患者被诊断为CD后,在长期使用硫唑嘌呤(AZA)治疗期间,先后发展出三种不同的癌变。该患者在使用AZA治疗3年后出现低级别宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN 1;第10年),在治疗7年后(第14年)停用AZA 10个月后出现结膜鳞状细胞癌,以及在约9.2年累计AZA治疗后(第17年)出现皮肤基底细胞癌。在第18年,CD复发并重新开始AZA治疗,此时检测到CIN 2(约10.2年累计AZA治疗)。在第18年,AZA治疗被终止。不到一年(第19年),诊断出宫颈鳞状细胞癌。第23年时,CD得到控制,未见肿瘤记录。通过PCR检测到宫颈和结膜恶性肿瘤中存在高危型HPV16 DNA,并通过桑格测序确认。免疫组化分析显示高Ki-67和p16表达以及CD4+细胞占优,支持这些肿瘤与HPV16的关联。CIN 2与侵袭性宫颈鳞状细胞癌(CSCC)之间的时间间隔较短,这在临床上较为罕见。虽然这一时间点与慢性免疫抑制治疗重合,但目前尚无法直接推断因果关系;然而,患者的慢性免疫失调和免疫抑制可能为这些肿瘤的形成提供了贡献因素。结论:该病例突显了克罗恩病、长期免疫抑制治疗和HPV16感染在时间上的共存。这些发现强调了在慢性免疫抑制治疗期间对肠外癌症进行警惕性监测、定期筛查以及HPV 疫苗接种的重要性。局限性与证据强度:CIN 2与侵袭性CSCC之间的时间间隔较短,这在临床上较为罕见。虽然这一时间点与慢性免疫抑制治疗重合,但目前尚无法直接推断因果关系;然而,患者的慢性免疫失调和免疫抑制可能为这些肿瘤的形成提供了贡献因素。

问题与背景

核心方法

主要结果
在第18年,CD复发并重新开始AZA治疗,此时检测到CIN 2(约10.2年累计AZA治疗)。不到一年(第19年),诊断出宫颈鳞状细胞癌。第23年时,CD得到控制,未见肿瘤记录。

Multiple Extraintestinal Malignancies and HPV16-Associated Neoplasms in Crohn’s Disease Under Prolonged Immunosuppression: A Case Report

Authors: Neuzenir da Silva de Abreu Oliveira, Karina França, Anna Karoline Fausto da Silva, Bernardo Acacio Santini Pereira, Ana Luiza Miguez Rodrigues, Juliana Werneck Coelho, Carlos Roberto Alves, Geovane Dias-Lopes, Susana Cristina Aide Viviani Fialho
Original abstract (English)
Background: Crohn’s disease (CD) is a chronic inflammatory disease of the gastrointestinal tract with an increased risk for malignancy, particularly in patients undergoing immunosuppressive therapy. Case Presentation: This report describes the 23-year clinical course of a 20-year-old woman diagnosed with CD who, under prolonged therapy with azathioprine (AZA), developed three distinct carcinomas. The patient sequentially developed low-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN 1; Year 10; 3 years of AZA), a conjunctival squamous cell carcinoma (Year 14; 7 years of AZA; followed by a 10-month AZA pause), and a cutaneous basal cell carcinoma (Year 17; ~9.2 cumulative years of AZA). After CD recurrence and AZA resumption, CIN 2 was detected (Year 18; ~10.2 cumulative years of AZA). In the 18th year, AZA therapy was discontinued. Within less than one year (Year 19), cervical squamous cell carcinoma was diagnosed. In Year 23, CD was controlled, without neoplasms documented. High-risk HPV16 DNA was detected in cervical and conjunctival malignancies by PCR and confirmed by Sanger sequencing. Immunohistochemical analysis showed high Ki-67 and p16 expression and CD4+ cell predominance, supporting an association of HPV16 in these neoplasms. The short interval between CIN 2 and invasive CSCC detection is unusual. While this coincided with chronic immunosuppression, direct causality currently cannot be inferred; however, the patient’s chronic immune dysregulation and immunosuppression may be contributing factors. Conclusions: This case highlights the temporal co-occurrence of Crohn’s disease, prolonged immunosuppression, and HPV16 infection. These findings raise the need for vigilant extraintestinal cancer surveillance, regular screening, and HPV vaccination during chronic immunosuppression.

宫颈病变中E6/E7 mRNA表达与病毒DNA载量的比较评估:多重高危型人乳头瘤病毒感染

Comparative Evaluation of E6/E7 mRNA Expression and Viral DNA Load in Cervical Lesions With Multiple High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection.
作者： Yan Zhou, Yan Feng, Yunfeng Zhang, Yang Yu, Zeng Yuan, Ruijiao Zhao, Yue Wang, Feng Zhou, Rujia Fan, Wenxin Zheng
摘要中文翻译背景:在宫颈筛查中,多重高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)感染经常被检测到,但其生物学相互作用和临床意义仍不清楚。由于HPV DNA检测仅表明病毒存在而非转录活性,本探索性研究评估了病毒载量、基因型特异性E6/E7 mRNA表达、定位及随访情况。方法:在12 000份用于hrHPV检测的宫颈标本中,有598份呈阳性,其中72份显示多重基因型。使用BioPerfectus Multiplex Real-Time检测方法评估病毒载量。选取36份双阳性HPV16 + 52或HPV16 + 58的组织可用子集,通过双色RNAscope技术分析基因型特异性E6/E7 mRNA表达和定位。诊断分为良性、低级别鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)或高级别鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)。病毒载量和RNAscope分析使用不完全匹配的子集,并被解释为互补而非配对分析。结果:病毒载量升高(≥4.00 log10 copies/10 000 cells)与HSIL相关。在基因型水平分析中,弥漫性(+++) E6/E7 mRNA表达在HSIL中的发生率高于病毒载量升高(95.2% vs 55.5%,P = .01)。RNAscope阴性病例多为良性或LSIL,且无进展。基因型特异性信号在空间上相互分离,重叠较少。双阳性细胞的RNA信号比相邻单阳性细胞更少(P < .001)。更高的E6/E7 mRNA表达可能与LSIL进展有关,但事件有限。结论:在多重hrHPV感染中,转录活性可能比单独的病毒DNA负担更能反映宫颈病变的生物学特性。基因型特异性E6/E7 mRNA表达可能补充HPV DNA载量。空间上分离的信号和双阳性细胞中信号强度的降低提示可能有感染hrHPV基因型之间的相互作用,但需要在完全匹配的基因型中进行前瞻性验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在宫颈筛查中,多重高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)感染经常被检测到,但其生物学相互作用和临床意义仍不清楚。
设计与对象
在12 000份用于hrHPV检测的宫颈标本中,有598份呈阳性,其中72份显示多重基因型。选取36份双阳性HPV16 + 52或HPV16 + 58的组织可用子集,通过双色RNAscope技术分析基因型特异性E6/E7 mRNA表达和定位。
核心方法
使用BioPerfectus Multiplex Real-Time检测方法评估病毒载量。诊断分为良性、低级别鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)或高级别鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)。病毒载量和RNAscope分析使用不完全匹配的子集,并被解释为互补而非配对分析。
主要结果
病毒载量升高(≥4.00 log10 copies/10 000 cells)与HSIL相关。在基因型水平分析中,弥漫性(+++) E6/E7 mRNA表达在HSIL中的发生率高于病毒载量升高(95.2% vs 55.5%,P = .01)。RNAscope阴性病例多为良性或LSIL,且无进展。基因型特异性信号在空间上相互分离,重叠较少。双阳性细胞的RNA信号比相邻单阳性细胞更少(P < .001)。更高的E6/E7 mRNA表达可能与LSIL进展有关,但事件有限。
解释与创新
在多重hrHPV感染中,转录活性可能比单独的病毒DNA负担更能反映宫颈病变的生物学特性。基因型特异性E6/E7 mRNA表达可能补充HPV DNA载量。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,E6/E7 mRNA表达可能为多重hrHPV感染相关宫颈病变进展提供额外的见解,可能有助于改善诊断和预后方法。
局限与证据强度
由于病毒载量和RNAscope分析使用了不完全匹配的子集,这可能限制了所观察到的关联强度。由于研究的探索性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Comparative Evaluation of E6/E7 mRNA Expression and Viral DNA Load in Cervical Lesions With Multiple High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection.

Authors: Yan Zhou, Yan Feng, Yunfeng Zhang, Yang Yu, Zeng Yuan, Ruijiao Zhao, Yue Wang, Feng Zhou, Rujia Fan, Wenxin Zheng
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Multiple high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections are frequently detected in cervical screening, but their biological interactions and clinical significance remain unclear. Because HPV DNA detection indicates viral presence but not transcriptional activity, this exploratory study evaluated viral load, genotype-specific E6/E7 mRNA expression, localization, and follow-up. METHODS: Among 12 000 cervical specimens tested for hrHPV, 598 were positive and 72 showed multiple genotypes. Viral load was assessed using the BioPerfectus Multiplex Real-Time assay. A tissue-available subset of 36 dual HPV16 + 52 or HPV16 + 58 cases was analyzed by duplex RNAscope for genotype-specific E6/E7 mRNA expression and localization. Diagnoses were categorized as benign, low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL), or high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL). Viral load and RNAscope analyses used incompletely matched subsets and were interpreted as complementary, not paired. RESULTS: Elevated viral load (≥4.00 log10 copies/10 000 cells) was associated with HSIL. In cohort-level analyses, diffuse (+++) E6/E7 mRNA expression was more frequent in HSIL than elevated viral load (95.2% vs 55.5%, P = .01). RNAscope-negative cases were mostly benign or LSIL and showed no progression. Genotype-specific signals were spatially distinct, with rare overlap. Dual-positive cells showed fewer RNA signals than adjacent single-positive cells (P < .001). Higher E6/E7 mRNA expression showed possible association with LSIL progression, but events were limited. CONCLUSIONS: In multiplex hrHPV infection, transcriptional activity may better reflect cervical lesion biology than viral DNA burden alone. Genotype-specific E6/E7 mRNA expression may complement HPV DNA load. Spatially distinct signals and reduced signal intensity in rare dual-positive cells suggest possible interactions between coinfecting hrHPV genotypes, but require prospective validation in fully matched cohorts.

Question and background
Multiple high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infections are frequently detected in cervical screening, but their biological interactions and clinical significance remain unclear.
Design and population
Among 12 000 cervical specimens tested for hrHPV, 598 were positive and 72 showed multiple genotypes. A tissue-available subset of 36 dual HPV16 + 52 or HPV16 + 58 cases was analyzed by duplex RNAscope for genotype-specific E6/E7 mRNA expression and localization.
Core methods
Viral load was assessed using the BioPerfectus Multiplex Real-Time assay. Diagnoses were categorized as benign, low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL), or high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL). Viral load and RNAscope analyses used incompletely matched subsets and were interpreted as complementary, not paired.
Main results
Elevated viral load (≥4.00 log10 copies/10 000 cells) was associated with HSIL. In cohort-level analyses, diffuse (+++) E6/E7 mRNA expression was more frequent in HSIL than elevated viral load (95.2% vs 55.5%, P = .01). RNAscope-negative cases were mostly benign or LSIL and showed no progression. Genotype-specific signals were spatially distinct, with rare overlap. Dual-positive cells showed fewer RNA signals than adjacent single-positive cells (P < .001). Higher E6/E7 mRNA expression showed possible association with LSIL progression, but events were limited.
Interpretation and novelty
In multiplex hrHPV infection, transcriptional activity may better reflect cervical lesion biology than viral DNA burden alone. Genotype-specific E6/E7 mRNA expression may complement HPV DNA load.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that E6/E7 mRNA expression could provide additional insights into the progression of cervical lesions associated with multiple hrHPV infections, potentially improving diagnostic and prognostic approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used incompletely matched subsets for viral load and RNAscope analyses, which may limit the strength of the association observed. The evidence is moderate due to the exploratory nature and potential for confounding factors.

高危型HPV整合通过HPV特异性条带转录因子重新编程顺式调控架构

High-risk HPV integration reprograms cis-regulatory architecture through HPV-specific stripe transcription factors
作者： Canhui Cao, Zhi-Xian Zhu, Miao-Chun Xu, Shi-Tong Lin, Xing-Yu Huang, Yuan-Hui Sun, Ting Peng, Xiao-Jie Liu, Ya-Shi Xu, Tian Chu
摘要中文翻译背景:高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)DNA整合到宿主 基因组中会促进hrHPV相关癌变中的持续病毒感染和恶性进展,但与整合相关的顺式调控程序仍不完全明确,限制了潜在治疗脆弱性的识别。我们旨在表征与hrHPV整合相关的调控架构,并评估其在临床前模型中的功能相关性。方法:建立了携带整合HPV16上游调控区-E6/E7序列的人类宫颈上皮类器官和一个HPV16整合的小鼠模型(14 E2.2 HPV16-int)。通过整合病毒整合图谱、基序富集分析、染色质可及性与组蛋白修饰分析、三维染色质相互作用分析、批量和单细胞转录组学、空间转录组学以及患者来源的类器官/异种移植模型,评估与hrHPV整合相关的调控变化和药物干预。结果:携带hrHPV整合的人类上皮类器官和小鼠在体外和体内均重现了逐渐恶化的恶性表型。在宫颈癌和头颈癌数据集中,hrHPV整合热点富含一组定义的25种HPV特异性条带转录因子(HSTFs)的基序。在hrHPV阳性上皮癌轨迹中,HSTF基序密度越高,染色质可及性、活跃的组蛋白标记、超级增强子-启动子相互作用以及HSTF调控活性均相应增强。空间和单细胞多组学分析进一步证实,HSTF的激活是由hrHPV整合触发的,这种激活可以在体内通过HSTF抑制被阻断。结论:这些发现揭示了与hrHPV整合相关的顺式调控网络,该网络促进染色质重塑和转录激活,从而推动HPV驱动的癌变。针对HSTF相关的调控程序可能代表一种治疗高危型HPV整合的癌前病变和癌症的有前景的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)DNA整合到宿主 基因组中会促进hrHPV相关癌变中的持续病毒感染和恶性进展,但与整合相关的顺式调控程序仍不完全明确,限制了潜在治疗脆弱性的识别。
设计与对象
建立了携带整合HPV16上游调控区-E6/E7序列的人类宫颈上皮类器官和一个HPV16整合的小鼠模型(14 E2.2 HPV16-int),以评估与整合相关的调控变化和药物干预。
核心方法
通过整合病毒整合图谱、基序富集分析、染色质可及性与组蛋白修饰分析、三维染色质相互作用分析、批量和单细胞转录组学、空间转录组学以及患者来源的类器官/异种移植模型,评估与hrHPV整合相关的调控变化。
主要结果
携带hrHPV整合的人类上皮类器官和小鼠在体外和体内均重现了逐渐恶化的恶性表型。在宫颈癌和头颈癌数据集中,hrHPV整合热点富含一组定义的25种HPV特异性条带转录因子(HSTFs)的基序。在hrHPV阳性上皮癌轨迹中,HSTF基序密度越高,染色质可及性、活跃的组蛋白标记、超级增强子-启动子相互作用以及HSTF调控活性均相应增强。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了与hrHPV整合相关的顺式调控网络,该网络促进染色质重塑和转录激活,从而推动HPV驱动的癌变。
科研与公卫意义
针对HSTF相关的调控程序可能代表一种治疗高危型HPV整合的癌前病变和癌症的有前景的策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于临床前模型,未包括人类临床试验,这限制了研究结果在人类人群中的直接适用性。由于依赖体外和体内模型,证据强度中等。

High-risk HPV integration reprograms cis-regulatory architecture through HPV-specific stripe transcription factors

Authors: Canhui Cao, Zhi-Xian Zhu, Miao-Chun Xu, Shi-Tong Lin, Xing-Yu Huang, Yuan-Hui Sun, Ting Peng, Xiao-Jie Liu, Ya-Shi Xu, Tian Chu
Original abstract (English)
Background: The integration of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) DNA into the host genome contributes to persistent viral infection and malignant progression in hrHPV-related carcinomas, but the integration-associated cis-regulatory programs remain incompletely defined, limiting the identification of potential therapeutic vulnerabilities.We aimed to characterize the regulatory architecture associated with hrHPV integration and evaluate its functional relevance in preclinical models.Methods: Human cervical epithelial organoids carrying integrated HPV16 upstream regulatory region-E6/E7 sequences and an HPV16-integrated mouse model (14 E2.2 HPV16-int ) were established.Viral integration mapping, motif enrichment analysis, chromatin accessibility and histone modification profiling, three-dimensional chromatin interaction analysis, bulk and single-cell transcriptomics, spatial transcriptomics, and patient-derived organoid/xenograft models were integrated to assess integration-associated regulatory changes and pharmacological perturbation.Results: Human epithelial organoids and mice with hrHPV integration recapitulated progressive malignant phenotypes in vitro and in vivo.Across cervical and head and neck cancer datasets, hrHPV integration hotspots were enriched for motifs of a defined subset of 25 HPV-specific stripe transcription factors (HSTFs).Higher HSTF motif density was associated with increased chromatin accessibility, active histone marks, enhanced super-enhancer-promoter interactions, and elevated HSTF regulatory activity along hrHPV-positive epithelial cancer trajectories.Spatial and single-cell multi-omic analyses further confirmed that HSTF activation was triggered by hrHPV integration, which could be blocked by HSTF inhibition in vivo.Conclusions: These findings reveal an hrHPV integration-associated cis-regulatory network that promotes chromatin remodeling and transcriptional activation in HPVdriven carcinogenesis.Targeting HSTF-associated regulatory program may represent a promising therapeutic strategy for hrHPV-integrated precancerous lesions and cancers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The integration of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) DNA into the host genome contributes to persistent viral infection and malignant progression in hrHPV-related carcinomas, but the integration-associated cis-regulatory programs remain incompletely defined, limiting the identification of potential therapeutic vulnerabilities.
Design and population
Human cervical epithelial organoids carrying integrated HPV16 upstream regulatory region-E6/E7 sequences and an HPV16-integrated mouse model (14 E2.2 HPV16-int) were established to assess integration-associated regulatory changes and pharmacological perturbation.
Core methods
Viral integration mapping, motif enrichment analysis, chromatin accessibility and histone modification profiling, three-dimensional chromatin interaction analysis, bulk and single-cell transcriptomics, spatial transcriptomics, and patient-derived organoid/xenograft models were integrated to evaluate the regulatory changes associated with hrHPV integration.
Main results
Human epithelial organoids and mice with hrHPV integration recapitulated progressive malignant phenotypes in vitro and in vivo. Across cervical and head and neck cancer datasets, hrHPV integration hotspots were enriched for motifs of a defined subset of 25 HPV-specific stripe transcription factors (HSTFs). Higher HSTF motif density was associated with increased chromatin accessibility, active histone marks, enhanced super-enhancer-promoter interactions, and elevated HSTF regulatory activity along hrHPV-positive epithelial cancer trajectories.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings reveal an hrHPV integration-associated cis-regulatory network that promotes chromatin remodeling and transcriptional activation in HPV-driven carcinogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Targeting HSTF-associated regulatory programs may represent a promising therapeutic strategy for hrHPV-integrated precancerous lesions and cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on preclinical models and does not include human clinical trials, which limits the direct applicability of findings to human populations. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on in vitro and in vivo models.
