人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的存在而非年龄可识别外阴上皮内瘤变:一项847例患者的多变量分析及40岁和45岁年龄分界点敏感性检验

Presence of HPV infection, not age, identifies vulvar intraepithelial neoplasia: a 847-patient multivariable analysis with 40- and 45-year age-cutoff sensitivity testing
作者： Zi-Yang Qu, Yi-Fen Li, Xiao-Hui Yang, Qing-Jian Ye, Min-Juan Ye, Yue-Bo Yang
摘要中文翻译背景 外阴上皮内瘤变(VIN)是外阴鳞状细胞癌的前驱病变,具有两种生物学上不同的发病途径:HPV相关和HPV无关的疾病。在调整了HPV感染和多灶性下生殖道疾病后,年龄是否独立与VIN相关仍不清楚。方法 我们分析了2022年4月至2025年12月期间因阴道镜检查和外阴评估而就诊的847名女性,包括568名VIN患者和279名非VIN对照组。进行了单变量比较、按年龄分层的多变量逻辑回归分析(Enter方法;完整病例数n = 673)以及使用40岁和45岁分界点的敏感性分析。在仅限于高危病变(HSIL/VIN2–3 vs. 结果 单变量分析显示VIN患者年龄略轻(平均39.66 ± 11.95岁 vs. 41.51 ± 12.52岁),更常无症状,且HPV感染率更高(80.2% vs. 55.7%,在40岁和45岁分界点时P均<0.05)。在45岁分界点时,老年组并发宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)的比例更高(12.2% vs. 在仅限于HSIL的敏感性分析中,连续年龄仍不显著(P = 0.492),而HPV感染是一个强的独立危险因素(P < 0.05)。HPV感染在所有模型中均一致且独立地与VIN相关(总体调整OR为3.146,P < 0.001;分层aOR为2.812–3.859,所有P < 0.001)。结论 在主要模型中连续年龄的边缘统计学显著性反映了HPV感染和多灶性疾病积累的残留混杂因素,而非年龄本身的独立致病作用。外阴检查应依据HPV感染状态和并发的下生殖道肿瘤,而非随意的年龄分界点,特别是考虑到无症状VIN的高患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定HPV感染或年龄与外阴上皮内瘤变(VIN)之间的关联强度,因为VIN是外阴鳞状细胞癌的前驱病变,具有两种生物学上不同的发病途径:HPV相关和HPV无关的疾病。
设计与对象
在2022年4月至2025年12月期间因阴道镜检查和外阴评估而就诊的847名女性中进行了多变量分析,其中包括568名VIN患者和279名非VIN对照组。
核心方法
进行了单变量比较、按年龄分层的多变量逻辑回归分析(Enter方法;完整病例数n = 673)以及使用40岁和45岁分界点的敏感性分析,以评估年龄、HPV感染与VIN之间的关联。
主要结果
单变量分析显示VIN患者年龄略轻(平均39.66 ± 11.95岁 vs. 41.51 ± 12.52岁),更常无症状,且HPV感染率更高(80.2% vs. 55.7%,在40岁和45岁分界点时P均<0.05)。
解释与创新
在主要模型中连续年龄的边缘统计学显著性反映了HPV感染和多灶性疾病积累的残留混杂因素,而非年龄本身的独立致病作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,外阴检查应依据HPV感染状态和并发的下生殖道肿瘤,而非随意的年龄分界点,特别是考虑到无症状VIN的高患病率。
局限与证据强度
本研究设计包括单变量比较和多变量逻辑回归分析,可能在控制所有混杂因素方面存在局限,但鉴于HPV感染在所有模型中均一致且独立地与VIN相关,证据是强有力的。

Presence of HPV infection, not age, identifies vulvar intraepithelial neoplasia: a 847-patient multivariable analysis with 40- and 45-year age-cutoff sensitivity testing

Authors: Zi-Yang Qu, Yi-Fen Li, Xiao-Hui Yang, Qing-Jian Ye, Min-Juan Ye, Yue-Bo Yang
Original abstract (English)
Background Vulvar intraepithelial neoplasia (VIN) is a precursor of vulvar squamous cell carcinoma with two biologically distinct pathways: HPV-associated and HPV-independent disease. Whether chronological age is independently associated with VIN after adjusting for HPV infection and multifocal lower genital tract disease remains unclear. Methods ​We analyzed 847 women referred for colposcopy and vulvar evaluation between April 2022 and December 2025, including 568 VIN patients and 279 non-VIN controls. Univariable comparisons, age-stratified multivariable logistic regression (Enter method; complete-case n = 673), and sensitivity analyses using 40-year and 45-year cutoffs were performed. A further sensitivity analysis was restricted to high-grade lesions (HSIL/VIN2–3 vs. Results Univariable analysis showed that VIN patients were slightly younger (mean 39.66 ± 11.95 vs. 41.51 ± 12.52 years), more frequently asymptomatic, and had a higher HPV infection rate (80.2% vs. 55.7%, both P 0.05 at 40-year and 45-year cutoffs). At the 45-year cutoff, concurrent CIN was more frequent in the older group (12.2% vs. In the HSIL-only sensitivity analysis, continuous age remained non-significant (P = 0.492), while HPV infection was a strong independent risk factor (P 0.05). HPV infection was consistently and independently associated with VIN across all models (overall adjusted OR 3.146, P < 0.001; stratified aOR 2.812–3.859, all P < 0.001). Conclusions The marginal statistical significance of continuous age in the primary model reflects residual confounding by HPV infection and multifocal disease accumulation rather than an independent pathogenic role of chronological age. Vulvar inspection should be guided by HPV infection status and concurrent lower genital tract neoplasia rather than arbitrary age cutoffs, particularly given the high prevalence of asymptomatic VIN.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine whether HPV infection or age is more strongly associated with vulvar intraepithelial neoplasia (VIN), as VIN is a precursor of vulvar squamous cell carcinoma with two biologically distinct pathways: HPV-associated and HPV-independent disease.
Design and population
A multivariable analysis was conducted on 847 women referred for colposcopy and vulvar evaluation between April 2022 and December 2025, including 568 VIN patients and 279 non-VIN controls.
Core methods
Univariable comparisons, age-stratified multivariable logistic regression (Enter method; complete-case n = 673), and sensitivity analyses using 40-year and 45-year cutoffs were performed to assess the association between age, HPV infection, and VIN.
Main results
Univariable analysis showed VIN patients were slightly younger (mean 39.66 ± 11.95 vs. 41.51 ± 12.52 years), more frequently asymptomatic, and had a higher HPV infection rate (80.2% vs. 55.7%, both P < 0.05 at 40-year and 45-year cutoffs).
Interpretation and novelty
The marginal statistical significance of continuous age in the primary model reflects residual confounding by HPV infection and multifocal disease accumulation rather than an independent pathogenic role of chronological age.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that vulvar inspection should be guided by HPV infection status and concurrent lower genital tract neoplasia rather than arbitrary age cutoffs, particularly given the high prevalence of asymptomatic VIN.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design, which included univariable comparisons and multivariable logistic regression, may have limitations in controlling for all confounding factors, but the evidence is strong given the consistent and independent association of HPV infection with VIN across all models.

通过移动健康增强人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗沟通:来自护理人员和医疗保健提供者的可用性见解。

Enhancing Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Communication with Mobile Health: Usability Insights from Caregivers and Health Care Providers.
作者： Elizabeth Reisher, Evangel Anyanwu, Jonathan Figliomeni, Adrien Honcoop, Ellen K Kerns, Russell Mcculloh, Martina A Clarke
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗在预防癌症方面有效,但其接种率仍不理想,尤其是在农村和医疗资源不足的人群中。数字通信工具如移动健康(mHealth)应用程序有潜力改善获取准确疫苗信息的途径,并支持共同决策。研究目的:本研究旨在评估一种新开发的mHealth应用程序在与美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的HPV 疫苗网站进行比较时的可用性、可接受性和感知有用性。研究方法:采用混合方法设计,依据健康信念模型和mHealth敏捷开发与评估生命周期,护理人员和医疗保健提供者被随机分配以交替顺序探索CDC网站和mHealth应用程序。参与者在使用每种工具回答HPV 疫苗相关问题后,完成了mHealth应用程序可用性问卷、信任倾向量表和NASA任务负荷指数(NASA-TLX)。在每次会话后,还通过定性问题收集关于导航和可读性的额外反馈。转录文本采用归纳主题分析法进行分析。研究结果:定量结果显示,两种工具在易用性、所需努力程度以及对工具的信任度方面没有显著差异。定性主题分析表明,参与者赞赏应用程序的简洁导航、定制内容和良好的可读性。相比之下,CDC网站被认为具有可信度,但在寻找细致或特定的疫苗信息时往往难以导航。研究结论:与现有的基于网络的资源相比,采用以用户为中心设计原则开发的定制mHealth工具可能更能满足护理人员和医疗保健提供者的信息和可用性需求。这些发现支持进一步开发和整合mHealth工具到疫苗沟通策略中,以增强HPV 疫苗的沟通效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过评估一种新开发的mHealth应用程序与美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的HPV 疫苗网站的可用性,解决HPV 人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)疫苗在农村和医疗资源不足人群中接种率不理想的问题。
设计与对象
本研究采用混合方法设计,涉及护理人员和医疗保健提供者,他们被随机分配以交替顺序使用CDC网站和mHealth应用程序。
核心方法
本研究采用健康信念模型和mHealth敏捷开发与评估生命周期,结合定量和定性方法评估可用性、可接受性和感知有用性。
主要结果
定量结果显示两种工具在易用性和所需努力程度上没有差异,而定性反馈则突出了应用程序的简洁导航和定制内容。
解释与创新
本研究发现,定制的mHealth工具可能比现有的基于网络的资源更能满足护理人员和医疗保健提供者的信息和可用性需求,支持进一步开发此类工具。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,mHealth工具可以增强HPV 疫苗的沟通,可能提高疫苗的接种率,尤其是在医疗资源不足的人群中。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括依赖自我报告测量以及随机化过程中可能存在偏倚,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍适用性。

Enhancing Human Papillomavirus Vaccine Communication with Mobile Health: Usability Insights from Caregivers and Health Care Providers.

Authors: Elizabeth Reisher, Evangel Anyanwu, Jonathan Figliomeni, Adrien Honcoop, Ellen K Kerns, Russell Mcculloh, Martina A Clarke
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Despite the effectiveness of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in preventing cancer, uptake remains suboptimal, especially among rural and underserved populations. Digital communication tools such as mobile health (mHealth) applications have the potential to improve access to accurate vaccine information and support shared decision-making. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to assess the usability, acceptability, and perceived usefulness of a newly developed mHealth app for HPV vaccine communication in comparison to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention's (CDC) HPV vaccine Web site. METHODS: Using a mixed-methods design and guided by the Health Belief Model and the mHealth Agile Development and Evaluation Lifecycle, caregivers, and health care providers were randomized to explore both the CDC Web site and the mHealth app in alternating order. Participants were asked to complete a session answering HPV vaccine questions with each tool and completed the mHealth App Usability Questionnaire, Propensity to Trust, and NASA Task Load Index (NASA-TLX) after each session. Qualitative questions were asked after each session to capture additional feedback on items such as navigation and readability. Transcripts were analyzed using inductive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Quantitative results showed no difference between the tools in terms of being easy to use, requiring similar amounts of effort, and trust in the tool. Qualitative themes revealed that participants appreciated the app's streamlined navigation, tailored content, and readability. In contrast, the CDC Web site was seen as credible but often difficult to navigate, especially when searching for nuanced or specific vaccine information. CONCLUSION: Tailored mHealth tools developed with user-centered design principles may better meet the informational and usability needs of caregivers and health care providers compared with existing web-based resources. These findings support further development and integration of mHealth tools into vaccine communication strategies to enhance HPV vaccine communication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the suboptimal uptake of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine, especially among rural and underserved populations, by evaluating the usability of a newly developed mHealth app compared to the CDC's HPV vaccine Web site.
Design and population
A mixed-methods design was used, involving caregivers and health care providers who were randomized to use both the CDC Web site and the mHealth app in alternating order.
Core methods
The study employed the Health Belief Model and the mHealth Agile Development and Evaluation Lifecycle, using quantitative and qualitative methods to assess usability, acceptability, and perceived usefulness.
Main results
Quantitative results indicated no difference in ease of use or effort required between the tools, while qualitative feedback highlighted the app's streamlined navigation and tailored content.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that tailored mHealth tools may better meet the informational and usability needs of caregivers and health care providers compared to existing web-based resources, supporting further development of such tools.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that mHealth tools can enhance HPV vaccine communication, potentially improving vaccine uptake, particularly among underserved populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its reliance on self-reported measures and the potential for bias in the randomization process, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

乳腺癌活检中人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒的存在作为潜在风险因素

Presence of Human Papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr Virus in Breast Cancer Biopsies as Potential Risk Factors
作者： Adriana Pesci-Feltri, Andreína Fernándes, Ávila Maira, Marco López, María Correnti, Marla Ladera, Andreina Fernandes, Maira Ávila
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乳腺癌是委内瑞拉女性人群中癌症相关死亡的主要原因。许多促进其发生的危险因素已被报道;然而,人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒在过去几十年中被关联为这种恶性肿瘤发展的潜在危险因素。
设计与对象
从在加拉加斯大学医院接受手术的乳腺癌患者和乳腺良性病理患者中收集新鲜活检样本。
核心方法
使用INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra商业试剂盒(Innogenetics)检测人乳头瘤病毒,使用Epstein-Barr病毒BMLF1商业试剂盒(Maxim Biotech,Inc.)检测Epstein-Barr病毒基因组。
主要结果
63.6和13.6%的乳腺癌病例分别对人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒DNA呈阳性,而良性病理样本中每种病毒的阳性率均为4.5%;42.90%的乳腺癌样本存在低和高致癌风险的人乳头瘤病毒基因型混合感染。
解释与创新
我们可以建议人乳头瘤病毒和Epstein-Barr病毒是乳腺癌的重要风险因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来研究人乳头瘤病毒在乳腺癌发展中的作用提供信息,并强调进一步研究其作为风险因素潜力的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Presence of Human Papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr Virus in Breast Cancer Biopsies as Potential Risk Factors

Authors: Adriana Pesci-Feltri, Andreína Fernándes, Ávila Maira, Marco López, María Correnti, Marla Ladera, Andreina Fernandes, Maira Ávila
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Breast cancer represents the leading cause of cancer-related death in the Venezuelan female population.Many risk factors favoring its appearance have been reported; however, human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus have been associated in the past few decades as potential risk factors in the development of this malignancy.Objective: To detect the presence of human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus in breast cancer biopsies in order to establish a possible link between infection with these viral agents and the development of this pathology.Methods: Fresh biopsies were collected from patients with breast cancer and patients with breast benign pathology attending the Hospital Universitario de Caracas for surgery.Human papillomavirus detection was made using the INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra commercial kit (Innogenetics), and Epstein-Barr virus genome was detected with Epstein-Barr Virus BMLF1 commercial kit (Maxim Biotech, Inc.).Results: 63.6 and 13.6% of breast cancer cases were positive for human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus DNA, respectively, whereas the benign pathology samples had 4.5% positivity for each one of the viruses; 42.90% of breast cancer samples had mixed infection with low and high oncogenic risk human papillomavirus genotypes.Conclusion: We can suggest that human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus are important risk factors for breast cancer; however, studies allowing for the role of these viruses in the development of the disease to be elucidated are required.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Breast cancer represents the leading cause of cancer-related death in the Venezuelan female population. Many risk factors favoring its appearance have been reported; however, human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus have been associated in the past few decades as potential risk factors in the development of this malignancy.
Design and population
Fresh biopsies were collected from patients with breast cancer and patients with breast benign pathology attending the Hospital Universitario de Caracas for surgery.
Core methods
Human papillomavirus detection was made using the INNO-LIPA ® HPV Genotyping Extra commercial kit (Innogenetics), and Epstein-Barr virus genome was detected with Epstein-Barr Virus BMLF1 commercial kit (Maxim Biotech, Inc.).
Main results
63.6 and 13.6% of breast cancer cases were positive for human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus DNA, respectively, whereas the benign pathology samples had 4.5% positivity for each one of the viruses; 42.90% of breast cancer samples had mixed infection with low and high oncogenic risk human papillomavirus genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
We can suggest that human papillomavirus and Epstein-Barr virus are important risk factors for breast cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the role of human papillomavirus in breast cancer development and highlight the need for further investigation into its potential as a risk factor.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

挪威宫颈上皮内瘤变2/3级(CIN2/3)锥切术后CIN3+长期风险分层:比较人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测与细胞学/HPV16/18/45 mRNA联合检测策略

Post-Treatment Surveillance After Conisation for CIN2/3 in Norway: Long-Term CIN3+ Risk Stratification Comparing HPV DNA Testing with Cytology/HPV16/18/45 mRNA Co-Testing
作者： Sveinung Wergeland Sørbye, Bente Marie Falang, Mona Antonsen, Elin Richardsen
摘要中文翻译我们对2016年至2020年间接受锥切术治疗宫颈上皮内瘤变2/3级(CIN2/3)的2136名女性进行了回顾性区域研究,比较了锥切术后不同检测策略。利用锥切术后90至548天内首次符合资格的访问结果,我们评估了细胞学检测、人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测以及HPV16/18/45 E6/E7信使RNA(mRNA)检测,分别单独使用和联合使用。我们还评估了一种探索性转诊策略,该策略借鉴了《应对高级HPV诊断需求》(ATHENA)研究。组织学结果通过2025年确认,中位数行政随访时间为7.24年。CIN3+(CIN3、原位腺癌或侵袭性宫颈癌)在74名女性中被记录(3.5%)。对于CIN3+,HPV DNA检测的敏感性为93.2%,特异性为78.0%,阳性率为24.4%。将mRNA阳性结果与高级别+细胞学结果结合,CIN3+的敏感性从60.8%提高到82.4%,特异性为92.1%,阳性率为10.4%。将ASC-US+细胞学结果与mRNA阳性结果结合,达到了最高的观察到的CIN3+敏感性(95.9%),特异性为73.5%。基于ATHENA研究的策略实现了90.5%的敏感性和83.1%的特异性,其中19.4%被归类为转诊阳性。在细胞学结果为可疑或低级别的情况下,mRNA阳性者中有18.6%被记录为CIN3+,而mRNA阴性者中仅2.5%被记录为CIN3+。细胞学和基因型特异性mRNA联合检测可能有助于锥切术后风险分层。需要前瞻性评估以确定安全的锥切术后随访间隔。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估不同锥切术后检测策略在识别宫颈上皮内瘤变2/3级(CIN2/3)长期CIN3+风险方面的有效性,以应对锥切术后改进风险分层方法的需求。
设计与对象
对2016年至2020年间接受锥切术治疗CIN2/3的2136名女性进行了回顾性区域研究。组织学结果通过2025年确认,中位数行政随访时间为7.24年。
核心方法
本研究利用锥切术后90至548天内首次符合资格的访问结果,分别单独评估了细胞学检测、人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测以及HPV16/18/45 E6/E7 mRNA联合检测,并评估了联合使用的效果。还评估了一种基于ATHENA研究的探索性转诊策略。
主要结果
CIN3+在74名女性中被记录(3.5%)。HPV DNA检测的敏感性为93.2%,特异性为78.0%,阳性率为24.4%。将ASC-US+细胞学结果与mRNA阳性结果结合,达到了最高的观察到的CIN3+敏感性(95.9%),特异性为73.5%。
解释与创新
作者认为,细胞学和基因型特异性mRNA联合检测可能有助于锥切术后风险分层,提供了一种更敏感的检测CIN3+的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于制定更有效的锥切术后随访策略,以降低宫颈癌的长期风险,从而改善长期监测并减少漏诊的可能性。
局限与证据强度
该研究为回顾性研究,缺乏前瞻性评估,这限制了所提出的锥切术后随访间隔的可信度。由于研究的观察性质和潜在偏倚,证据强度中等。

Post-Treatment Surveillance After Conisation for CIN2/3 in Norway: Long-Term CIN3+ Risk Stratification Comparing HPV DNA Testing with Cytology/HPV16/18/45 mRNA Co-Testing

Authors: Sveinung Wergeland Sørbye, Bente Marie Falang, Mona Antonsen, Elin Richardsen
Original abstract (English)
We compared surveillance strategies in a retrospective regional cohort of 2136 women who underwent conisation for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or 3 (CIN2/3) in 2016–2020. Using results from the first eligible visit 90–548 days after treatment, we evaluated cytology, human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing and HPV16/18/45 E6/E7 messenger RNA (mRNA) testing, individually and in combination. We also evaluated an exploratory referral strategy adapted from the Addressing the Need for Advanced HPV Diagnostics (ATHENA) study. Histological outcomes were ascertained through 2025. Median available administrative follow-up was 7.24 years. CIN3+ (CIN3, adenocarcinoma in situ or invasive cervical cancer) was recorded in 74 women (3.5%). For CIN3+, HPV DNA testing had 93.2% sensitivity, 78.0% specificity and 24.4% positivity. Adding mRNA positivity to high-grade+ cytology increased CIN3+ sensitivity from 60.8% to 82.4%, with 92.1% specificity and 10.4% test positivity. Combining ASC-US+ cytology with mRNA positivity achieved the highest observed CIN3+ sensitivity (95.9%), with 73.5% specificity. The ATHENA-derived strategy achieved 90.5% sensitivity and 83.1% specificity, with 19.4% classified as referral-positive. Among women with equivocal or low-grade cytology, CIN3+ was recorded in 18.6% with positive mRNA and 2.5% with negative mRNA. Cytology and genotype-specific mRNA co-testing may support risk stratification after conisation. Prospective evaluation is needed to establish safe surveillance intervals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of different post-treatment surveillance strategies for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2/3 (CIN2/3) in identifying long-term CIN3+ risk, addressing the need for improved risk stratification methods after conisation.
Design and population
A retrospective regional cohort of 2136 women who underwent conisation for CIN2/3 between 2016 and 2020 was studied. Histological outcomes were ascertained through 2025, with median administrative follow-up of 7.24 years.
Core methods
The study evaluated cytology, HPV DNA testing, and HPV16/18/45 E6/E7 mRNA co-testing, individually and in combination, using results from the first eligible visit 90–548 days after treatment. An exploratory referral strategy adapted from the ATHENA study was also assessed.
Main results
CIN3+ was recorded in 74 women (3.5%). HPV DNA testing had 93.2% sensitivity, 78.0% specificity, and 24.4% positivity. Combining ASC-US+ cytology with mRNA positivity achieved the highest observed CIN3+ sensitivity (95.9%) with 73.5% specificity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that cytology and genotype-specific mRNA co-testing may support risk stratification after conisation, offering a more sensitive approach for detecting CIN3+.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of more effective surveillance strategies for cervical cancer risk after treatment, potentially improving long-term monitoring and reducing the risk of missed diagnoses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and lacks prospective evaluation, which limits confidence in the proposed surveillance intervals. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature and potential for bias.

女性在进行宫颈镜检查时与活检证实的高危级宫颈病变(CIN2+)相关的因素:人乳头瘤病毒感染、疫苗接种状态和细胞学的作用

Factors associated with biopsy-confirmed high-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+) in women undergoing colposcopy: role of HPV infection, vaccination status, and cytology
作者： Maryam Vajihinejad, Abolfazl Taghinejad, Fatemeh Khaje-Bafghi, Mojgan Hajisafari Tafti, Mojgan Hajisafari-tafti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 高级别宫颈病变(CIN2+)是宫颈癌的重要前驱病变。本研究旨在评估在接受阴道镜检查的女性中与活检确诊CIN2+相关的因素,特别关注人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染和疫苗接种状态。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究包括了2014年1月至2024年1月期间接受阴道镜检查的246名女性。研究重点在于经活检确认的CIN2+女性,并将其与未出现此类病变的女性进行了比较。
核心方法
人乳头瘤病毒状态、疫苗接种状态、细胞学检查及临床特征进行了评估。采用单变量和多变量Firth惩罚逻辑回归分析以确定与CIN2+相关的因素。
主要结果
在分析中纳入的246名女性中,有50名(20.3%)通过活检确诊为CIN2+,其中包括37名(74.0%)CIN2、10名(20.0%)CIN3、2名(4.0%)原位腺癌以及1名(2.0%)浸润性宫颈癌。HPV状态和细胞学检查结果在CIN2+和CIN2−女性之间存在显著差异(两者p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
在多变量Firth惩罚逻辑回归分析中,HPV16/18感染(aOR为3.94;95% CI为1.70–9.74;p = 0.001)、缺乏HPV 疫苗接种(aOR为2.20;95% CI为1.03–4.65;p = 0.042),以及HSIL细胞学检查结果与NILM相比(aOR为2.84;95% CI为1.09–7.54;p = 0.031)独立地与CIN2+相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV16/18感染和HSIL细胞学检查是CIN2+发展的重要因素。这些信息可以用于指导宫颈癌筛查和预防策略,特别是在HPV 疫苗接种感染率较低的人群中。
局限与证据强度
疫苗接种与HPV暴露的时间关系未被记录,其与缺乏HPV 疫苗接种的关联可能反映了未测量的混杂因素。由于这些局限性,证据为中等。

Factors associated with biopsy-confirmed high-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+) in women undergoing colposcopy: role of HPV infection, vaccination status, and cytology

Authors: Maryam Vajihinejad, Abolfazl Taghinejad, Fatemeh Khaje-Bafghi, Mojgan Hajisafari Tafti, Mojgan Hajisafari-tafti
Original abstract (English)
Background High-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+) are important precursors of cervical cancer. HPV infection, cytological abnormalities, and HPV vaccination status may be associated with CIN2+. This study evaluated factors associated with biopsy-confirmed CIN2 + in women undergoing colposcopy, with particular focus on HPV infection and vaccination status. Methods In this retrospective cross-sectional study, 246 women undergoing colposcopy, from January 2014 to January 2024 were included. HPV was detected using the PaxView ® Kit. CIN2 + was defined as histopathologically confirmed CIN2, CIN3, adenocarcinoma in situ, or invasive cervical cancer. HPV status, vaccination status, cytology, and clinical characteristics were assessed. Univariable and multivariable Firth penalized logistic regression were used to identify factors associated with CIN2+. adjusted odds ratios (aOR) and 95% confidence intervals (CI) were calculated. Results Among 246 women included in the analysis, 50 (20.3%) had biopsy-confirmed CIN2+, including 37 (74.0%) with CIN2, 10 (20.0%) with CIN3, 2 (4.0%) with adenocarcinoma in situ, and 1 (2.0%) with invasive cervical cancer. HPV status and cytological findings differed significantly between women with CIN2 + and CIN2− (both p < 0.001). HPV16/18 was more frequent among women with CIN2 + than CIN2− (56.0% vs. 28.6%), whereas HPV-negative results were less frequent (18.0% vs. Similarly, HSIL cytology (38.0% vs. 10.7%) and lack of HPV vaccination (42.0% vs. 16.8%) were more common in the CIN2 + group. In multivariable Firth penalized logistic regression, HPV16/18 infection (aOR, 3.94; 95% CI, 1.70–9.74; p = 0.001), lack of HPV vaccination (aOR, 2.20; 95% CI, 1.03–4.65; p = 0.042), and HSIL cytology compared with NILM (aOR, 2.84; 95% CI, 1.09–7.54; p = 0.031) were independently associated with CIN2+. Age, marital status and other high-risk HPV genotypes were not significantly associated with CIN2+. Conclusion In this Iranian colposcopy population, HPV16/18 infection and HSIL cytology were independently associated with biopsy-confirmed CIN2+. Lack of HPV vaccination was also associated with higher odds of CIN2+, but this finding should be interpreted cautiously, as the timing of vaccination relative to HPV exposure was not recorded and the association may reflect unmeasured confounding parameters. Prospective studies focusing on vaccination timing and pre-vaccination HPV status are needed to clarify this association.

Question and background
Background High-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+) are important precursors of cervical cancer. This study aimed to evaluate factors associated with biopsy-confirmed CIN2+ in women undergoing colposcopy, with particular focus on HPV infection and vaccination status.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study included 246 women undergoing colposcopy from January 2014 to January 2024. The study focused on women with biopsy-confirmed CIN2+ and compared them with those without such lesions.
Core methods
HPV status, vaccination status, cytology, and clinical characteristics were assessed. Univariable and multivariable Firth penalized logistic regression were used to identify factors associated with CIN2+.
Main results
Among 246 women included in the analysis, 50 (20.3%) had biopsy-confirmed CIN2+, including 37 (74.0%) with CIN2, 10 (20.0%) with CIN3, 2 (4.0%) with adenocarcinoma in situ, and 1 (2.0%) with invasive cervical cancer. HPV status and cytological findings differed significantly between women with CIN2+ and CIN2− (both p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
In multivariable Firth penalized logistic regression, HPV16/18 infection (aOR, 3.94; 95% CI, 1.70–9.74; p = 0.001), lack of HPV vaccination (aOR, 2.20; 95% CI, 1.03–4.65; p = 0.042), and HSIL cytology compared with NILM (aOR, 2.84; 95% CI, 1.09–7.54; p = 0.031) were independently associated with CIN2+.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV16/18 infection and HSIL cytology are important factors in the development of CIN2+. This information can inform cervical cancer screening and prevention strategies, particularly in populations where HPV vaccination rates are low.
Limitations and evidence strength
The timing of vaccination relative to HPV exposure was not recorded, and the association with lack of HPV vaccination may reflect unmeasured confounding parameters. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

ASC-US/LSIL细胞学中非16/18型高危型人乳头瘤病毒感染与宫颈高级别上皮内瘤变的即时风险及初级分流策略:一项真实世界横断面研究

Immediate Risk of Cervical Precancer and Primary Triage Strategies for Women with Non- 16/18 High-Risk HPV Infection and ASC-US/LSIL Cytology: A Real-World Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Jun Ma, Jue Zhu, Linghui Weng, Yichen Chen, Liping Chen, Xiao Li, Jing Zhang
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 我们评估了在ASC-US(意义未明的非典型鳞状细胞)和LSIL(低级别鳞状上皮内病变)细胞学检查的女性中,非16/18型高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)基因型对宫颈高级别上皮内瘤变(CIN2+)的即时风险,并制定了基于风险的初级分流策略。我们还描述了2019年至2024年间中国宁波地区人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的流行病学特征。方法 根据定义的纳入标准,本研究纳入了2019年至2024年期间参与宁波宫颈癌筛查的511,133名女性。根据HPV和液基细胞学(TCT)检查结果进行阴道镜检查和组织病理学活检。我们使用最低归因、任意归因和分层归因模型来评估非16/18型hrHPV基因型的分布及其在ASC-US和LSIL细胞学检查下进展为CIN2+的即时风险。最后,我们通过整合最新的扩展HPV基因型证据,制定了一个基于风险的临床分流框架。结果 在511,133名接受筛查的女性中,有50,320名(9.84%)HPV阳性,其中HPV52、58和16是主要的基因型。非16/18型hrHPV占所有HPV阳性病例的84.18%(42,357/50,320)。在ASC-US和LSIL细胞学检查中,HPV31在所有非16/18型基因型中对CIN2+的即时风险最高。结论 尽管非16/18型hrHPV基因型在HPV阳性女性中占主导地位,但它们进展为CIN2+的即时风险高度异质。对非16/18型hrHPV感染采用统一的管理方案会浪费医疗资源。我们提出了一种三级、基于扩展基因型的分流策略,用于ASC-US/LSIL细胞学检查的女性:对高风险基因型(HPV31、33、52和58)直接进行阴道镜检查;对低风险基因型(HPV39、56、66和68)采取保守随访;对中等风险基因型则使用生物标志物(如DNA甲基化、p16/Ki-67双染色)或细胞学进行二次分流。该框架旨在进行初始的即时分流,不替代特定基因型的长期纵向监测监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估非16/18型高危型HPV基因型在ASC-US和LSIL细胞学检查的女性中对宫颈上皮内瘤变2级或以上(CIN2+)的即时风险,并制定基于风险的初级分流策略。
设计与对象
这项真实世界横断面研究纳入了2019年至2024年期间参与宁波宫颈癌筛查的511,133名女性,重点关注ASC-US和LSIL细胞学检查的女性。
核心方法
本研究使用最低归因、任意归因和分层归因模型来评估非16/18型hrHPV基因型的分布及其在ASC-US和LSIL细胞学检查下进展为CIN2+的即时风险。
主要结果
在511,133名接受筛查的女性中,有50,320名(9.84%)HPV阳性,其中HPV52、58和16为主要的基因型。非16/18型hrHPV占所有HPV阳性病例的84.18%(42,357/50,320)。
解释与创新
作者认为非16/18型hrHPV基因型在进展为CIN2+的即时风险上高度异质,并提出了针对ASC-US/LSIL细胞学检查女性的三级、基于扩展基因型的分流策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为管理ASC-US/LSIL细胞学检查女性的HPV感染制定基于风险的临床分流框架,可能有助于改善资源分配和患者预后。
局限与证据强度
由于研究是横断面设计且缺乏纵向数据,可能会影响对即时风险的解释。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Immediate Risk of Cervical Precancer and Primary Triage Strategies for Women with Non- 16/18 High-Risk HPV Infection and ASC-US/LSIL Cytology: A Real-World Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Jun Ma, Jue Zhu, Linghui Weng, Yichen Chen, Liping Chen, Xiao Li, Jing Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective We evaluated the immediate risk of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse (CIN2+) conferred by non-16/18 high-risk HPV (hrHPV) genotypes in women with atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance (ASC-US) and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) cytology, and developed a risk-based primary triage strategy. We also characterized the regional epidemiological features of HPV infection in Ningbo, China, from 2019 to 2024. Methods Based on defined eligibility criteria, this study enrolled 511,133 women participating in cervical cancer screening in Ningbo from 2019 to 2024. Colposcopy and histopathological biopsy were performed based on HPV and liquid-based cytology (TCT) findings. We used lowest-attribution (minimum), any-attribution, and hierarchical-attribution models to evaluate the distribution of non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes and their immediate risks of progressing to CIN2 + under ASC-US and LSIL cytology. Finally, we formulated a risk-stratified clinical triage framework by integrating recent evidence on extended HPV genotyping. Results Of 511,133 women screened, 50,320 (9.84%) were HPV-positive, with HPV52, 58, and 16 identified as the predominant genotypes. Non-16/18 hrHPV accounted for 84.18% (42,357/50,320) of all HPV-positive cases. Across both ASC-US and LSIL cytology, HPV31 conferred the highest immediate risk of CIN2 + among all non-16/18 genotypes. Conclusion Although non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes predominate among HPV-positive women, their immediate risks of CIN2 + are highly heterogeneous. A uniform management protocol for managing non-16/18 hrHPV infections wastes medical resources. We propose a three-tiered, extended genotyping-based triage strategy for women with ASC-US/LSIL cytology: direct colposcopy for high-risk genotypes (HPV31, 33, 52, and 58); conservative follow-up for low-risk genotypes (HPV39, 56, 66, and 68); and secondary triage using biomarkers (e.g., DNA methylation, p16/Ki-67 dual staining) or cytology for intermediate-risk genotypes. This framework is designed for initial, immediate triage and does not replace long-term longitudinal surveillance for specific genotypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immediate risk of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse (CIN2+) conferred by non-16/18 high-risk HPV genotypes in women with ASC-US and LSIL cytology, and to develop a risk-based primary triage strategy.
Design and population
This real-world cross-sectional study enrolled 511,133 women participating in cervical cancer screening in Ningbo, China, from 2019 to 2024, focusing on those with ASC-US and LSIL cytology.
Core methods
The study used lowest-attribution, any-attribution, and hierarchical-attribution models to assess the distribution of non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes and their immediate risks of progressing to CIN2+ under ASC-US and LSIL cytology.
Main results
Of the 511,133 women screened, 50,320 (9.84%) were HPV-positive, with HPV52, 58, and 16 being the predominant genotypes. Non-16/18 hrHPV accounted for 84.18% (42,357/50,320) of all HPV-positive cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that non-16/18 hrHPV genotypes have highly heterogeneous immediate risks of CIN2+ and proposed a three-tiered, extended genotyping-based triage strategy for women with ASC-US/LSIL cytology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of a risk-stratified clinical triage framework for managing HPV infections in women with ASC-US/LSIL cytology, potentially improving resource allocation and patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the lack of longitudinal data, which may affect the interpretation of immediate risk. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

阿布贾尼日利亚宫颈疾病谱中高风险人乳头瘤病毒检测:一项比较横断面研究

High-risk human papillomavirus detection across the cervical disease spectrum in Abuja, Nigeria: a comparative cross-sectional study
作者： Juliet Oluchukwu Offor, Ernest Orji, George Uchenna Eleje, Imran O. Morhason-Bello, Ayodele Omotoso, Offor, J. O., Eleje, G. U., Morhason-Bello, I. O.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)在阿布贾的宫颈疾病谱中仍是一个主要的公共卫生问题,但相关数据有限。
设计与对象
我们在阿布贾联邦医疗中心进行了基于医院的比较横断面研究,纳入了101名年龄≥25岁的女性:59名宫颈细胞学检查阴性(NILM)和42名宫颈病变患者,包括23例低度鳞状上皮内病变(LSIL)、8例高度鳞状上皮内病变(HSIL)和11例侵袭性宫颈癌。
核心方法
宫颈样本使用Atila ScreenFire检测方法进行检测,该方法报告四个合并的hrHPV目标组。
主要结果
在101名女性中,检测到37名(36.6%)hrHPV DNA:NILM组中30.5%(18/59)、LSIL组中26.1%(6/23)、HSIL组中50.0%(4/8)、侵袭性宫颈癌组中81.8%(9/11)(Fisher-Freeman-Halton精确p=0.005)。
解释与创新
Compared with NILM, invasive cervical cancer was associated with approximately tenfold higher odds of hrHPV detection (unadjusted odds ratio [OR] 10.25, 95% CI 2.01-52.28; age-adjusted OR 9.90, 95% CI 1.87-52.38).
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明侵袭性宫颈癌中hrHPV显著富集,并且非16/18目标组的检测率较高。
局限与证据强度
NILM组与病变组在种族、宗教和既往宫颈筛查方面存在显著差异,表明不同的转诊和筛查途径;因此,NILM组不应被视为基于人群的样本。

High-risk human papillomavirus detection across the cervical disease spectrum in Abuja, Nigeria: a comparative cross-sectional study

Authors: Juliet Oluchukwu Offor, Ernest Orji, George Uchenna Eleje, Imran O. Morhason-Bello, Ayodele Omotoso, Offor, J. O., Eleje, G. U., Morhason-Bello, I. O.
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer remains a major public health problem in Nigeria, yet data on high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) across the cervical disease spectrum are limited in Abuja. We conducted a hospital-based comparative cross-sectional study of 101 women aged [≥]25 years at Federal Medical Centre, Abuja: 59 with negative cytology (NILM) and 42 with cervical lesions, comprising 23 low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (LSIL), 8 high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL), and 11 invasive cervical cancers. Cervical specimens were tested using the Atila ScreenFire assay, which reports four pooled hrHPV target groups. hrHPV DNA was detected in 37/101 women (36.6%): 30.5% (18/59) with NILM, 26.1% (6/23) with LSIL, 50.0% (4/8) with HSIL, and 81.8% (9/11) with invasive cervical cancer (Fisher-Freeman-Halton exact p=0.005). Compared with NILM, invasive cervical cancer was associated with approximately tenfold higher odds of hrHPV detection (unadjusted odds ratio [OR] 10.25, 95% CI 2.01-52.28; age-adjusted OR 9.90, 95% CI 1.87-52.38). The HPV31/33/35/52/58 target group was detected most frequently overall (12.9%), followed by HPV16 and HPV18/45 (10.9% each), and HPV39/51/56/59/68 (5.9%). HPV16 was the most frequent target group in invasive cervical cancer (5/11). No individual target group was independently associated with lesion status. The NILM and lesion groups differed substantially in ethnicity, religion, and previous cervical screening, indicating different referral and screening pathways; therefore, the NILM group should not be considered population-based. These findings demonstrate marked enrichment of hrHPV in invasive cervical cancer and substantial detection of non-16/18 target groups. They support HPV-based screening, continued genotype surveillance, and larger multicentre studies using population-based comparison groups.
View research seven-element analysis

肥胖对宫颈癌前病变进展和人乳头瘤病毒持续性的影响差异:一项回顾性队列研究研究

Differential effect of obesity on cervical neoplastic progression and human papillomavirus persistence: A retrospective cohort study
作者： Cem Yağmur Özdemir, Nayif Çiçekli, Cevdet Doğu, Dağıstan Tolga Arıöz, Cem Yagmur Ozdemir, Nayif Cicekli, Dagistan Tolga Arioz
摘要中文翻译肥胖在人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)持续性以及宫颈癌前病变发展中的作用仍不完全明确。本研究评估了肥胖、吸烟和年龄对高危型HPV持续性以及高危宫颈病变(宫颈上皮内瘤变[CIN]2+)的独立影响,并评估肥胖是否能改善对CIN2+的预测能力。这项回顾性队列研究研究纳入了2023年12月至2025年12月期间接受高危型HPV检测的445名女性。将同一HPV基因型在至少两次间隔≥12个月的连续检测中均被检出定义为HPV持续性。根据体重指数将人群分为非肥胖(<30 kg/m2)和肥胖(≥30 kg/m2)。通过多变量逻辑回归分析确定HPV持续性和CIN2+的独立决定因素。使用受试者工作特征曲线分析比较包含年龄和吸烟的基线模型与加入肥胖的扩展模型。发现持续性HPV感染的女性有59例(13.3%)。吸烟是HPV持续性的强独立预测因素(调整比值比[aOR]:13.07;95%置信区间[CI]:6.54–26.09;p < 0.001),年龄增加也与持续性独立相关(aOR:每年1.04;95% CI:1.01–1.07;p = 0.023)。肥胖与HPV持续性无独立关联。CIN2+在95名女性中被检出(21.3%)。肥胖是CIN2+的独立预测因素(aOR:1.96;95% CI:1.14–3.38;p = 0.015),而吸烟和年龄则不是显著预测因素。肥胖的加入对CIN2+预测的判别能力有适度提升(曲线下面积[AUC] 0.545 vs. 吸烟和年龄是HPV持续性的关键决定因素,而肥胖似乎主要与宫颈癌前病变进展而非病毒持续性相关。AUC的适度增加表明肥胖对CIN2+风险的预测贡献有限但可测量。

Differential effect of obesity on cervical neoplastic progression and human papillomavirus persistence: A retrospective cohort study

Authors: Cem Yağmur Özdemir, Nayif Çiçekli, Cevdet Doğu, Dağıstan Tolga Arıöz, Cem Yagmur Ozdemir, Nayif Cicekli, Dagistan Tolga Arioz
Original abstract (English)
The role of obesity in human papillomavirus (HPV) persistence and the development of cervical neoplasia remains incompletely understood. This study evaluates the independent effects of obesity, smoking, and age on high-risk HPV persistence and the development of high-grade cervical lesions (cervical intraepithelial neoplasia [CIN]2+), and assesses whether obesity improves prediction of CIN2+. This retrospective cohort study included 445 women who underwent high-risk HPV testing between December 2023 and December 2025. HPV persistence was defined as detection of the same HPV genotype in at least two consecutive tests performed ≥ 12 months apart. Body mass index was categorized as non-obese (<30 kg/m2) or obese (≥30 kg/m2). Multivariable logistic regression analyses were performed to identify independent determinants of HPV persistence and CIN2+. Receiver operating characteristic curve analysis was used to compare a baseline model including age and smoking with an extended model additionally including obesity. Persistent HPV infection was identified in 59 women (13.3%). Smoking was a strong independent predictor of HPV persistence (adjusted odds ratio [aOR]: 13.07; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 6.54–26.09; p < 0.001), and increasing age was also independently associated with persistence (aOR: 1.04 per year; 95% CI: 1.01–1.07; p = 0.023). Obesity was not independently associated with HPV persistence. CIN2+ was detected in 95 women (21.3%). Obesity was independently associated with CIN2+ (aOR: 1.96; 95% CI: 1.14–3.38; p = 0.015), whereas smoking and age were not significant predictors. The addition of obesity modestly improved discrimination for CIN2+ prediction (area under the curve [AUC] 0.545 vs. Smoking and age are key determinants of HPV persistence, whereas obesity appears to be associated primarily with cervical neoplastic progression rather than viral persistence. The modest increase in AUC suggests limited but measurable predictive contribution of obesity to CIN2+ risk.

Question and background
The role of obesity in human papillomavirus (HPV) persistence and the development of cervical neoplasia remains incompletely understood.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study included 445 women who underwent high-risk HPV testing between December 2023 and December 2025.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression analyses were performed to identify independent determinants of HPV persistence and CIN2+.
Main results
Persistent HPV infection was identified in 59 women (13.3%). CIN2+ was detected in 95 women (21.3%).

Scientific and public-health significance
Smoking and age are key determinants of HPV persistence, whereas obesity appears to be associated primarily with cervical neoplastic progression rather than viral persistence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design.

基于新表位的癌症疫苗在HPV阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌中的应用:综述

Neoepitope-based cancer vaccines in human papillomavirus–negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: a review
作者： Giovanna Rossi, Francesco Carlo Tartaglia, Nerina Denaro, Michele Ghidini, Funda Goker, Ornella Garrone, Aldo Bruno Giannì, Massimo DEL FABBRO, Gianluca Martino Tartaglia, Paolo Bossi
摘要中文翻译HPV阴性的头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)即使经过多模式治疗治疗,仍具有显著的复发风险。由突变衍生的新表位为个性化疫苗接种提供了理论依据,但其临床相关性取决于靶点表达和克隆性、有效的抗原呈递以及功能性T细胞能否到达恶性细胞。本叙述性综述区分了直接HPV阴性HNSCC证据与HNSCC包容性项目、共享抗原对照组、靶点选择技术以及跨肿瘤研究。在33名接受手术切除的HPV阴性HNSCC患者中进行的随机I期TG4050研究支持了个性化新表位疫苗接种的可行性与免疫原性;然而,由于样本量小、复发事件少以及按协议分析,尚无法得出明确的疗效结论。互补的转化研究和跨肿瘤研究为抗原选择、疫苗递送以及联合策略提供了信息,但其在HPV阴性HNSCC中的可转移性仍不确定。我们探讨了预测靶点、自然人类白细胞抗原(HLA)呈递、T细胞识别、肿瘤细胞杀伤以及临床获益所需的独立证据。我们讨论了空间免疫分析以研究耐药性以及循环肿瘤DNA以监测疾病,同时强调这些方法在指导疫苗接种中的作用仍处于探索阶段。未来的研究应定义治疗的适用场景,评估疫苗接种是否能为当前标准治疗带来额外益处,报告制造过程中的损耗和首次给药时间,并将标准化的免疫测量与临床结果联系起来。基于新表位的疫苗接种在HPV阴性HNSCC中仍处于研究阶段,需要足够样本量的随机试验来确立其临床作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于基于新表位的癌症疫苗在HPV阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌(HNSCC)中的应用。核心问题是:在多模式治疗治疗下,HPV阴性HNSCC患者仍面临显著的复发风险,这些疫苗是否能改善其预后。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合分析,特别强调了在33名接受手术切除的HPV阴性HNSCC患者中进行的随机I期TG4050研究。该研究支持了个性化新表位疫苗接种的可行性与免疫原性,但由于样本量小、复发事件少以及按协议分析,尚缺乏足够的数据以得出明确的疗效结论。综述还指出,需要进行足够样本量的随机试验以确立新表位疫苗接种在HPV阴性HNSCC中的临床作用。
共识与结论
综述得出结论:由突变衍生的新表位为个性化疫苗接种在HPV阴性HNSCC中的应用提供了理论依据,但其临床相关性取决于靶点表达、克隆性、抗原呈递以及T细胞功能等因素。同时强调,尽管互补的转化研究和跨肿瘤研究为抗原选择、疫苗递送以及联合策略提供了见解,但其在HPV阴性HNSCC中的可转移性仍不确定。
争议与缺口
目前关于基于新表位的疫苗在HPV阴性HNSCC中的疗效证据有限,TG4050研究样本量过小,无法得出明确结论。此外,综述指出空间免疫分析和循环肿瘤DNA监测在指导疫苗接种中的作用仍处于探索阶段。综述还强调需要进一步研究治疗的适用场景、疫苗接种对标准治疗的额外益处以及免疫测量与临床结果之间的联系。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应定义治疗的适用场景,评估疫苗接种是否能为当前标准治疗带来额外益处,报告制造过程中的损耗和首次给药时间,并将标准化的免疫测量与临床结果联系起来。综述还建议,空间免疫分析和循环肿瘤DNA监测可能成为理解耐药性和疾病进展的有价值的工具,尽管它们在指导疫苗接种中的作用仍需进一步研究。

Neoepitope-based cancer vaccines in human papillomavirus–negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma: a review

Authors: Giovanna Rossi, Francesco Carlo Tartaglia, Nerina Denaro, Michele Ghidini, Funda Goker, Ornella Garrone, Aldo Bruno Giannì, Massimo DEL FABBRO, Gianluca Martino Tartaglia, Paolo Bossi
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) has a substantial risk of recurrence despite multimodal treatment. Mutation-derived neoepitopes provide a rationale for personalized vaccination, but their clinical relevance depends on target expression and clonality, effective antigen presentation, and access of functional T cells to malignant cells. This narrative review distinguishes direct HPV-negative HNSCC evidence from HNSCC-inclusive programs, shared-antigen comparators, target-selection technologies, and cross-tumor studies. The randomized phase I TG4050 study in 33 patients with resected HPV-negative HNSCC supports feasibility and immunogenicity; its small sample, few recurrence events, and per-protocol recurrence analysis preclude definitive efficacy conclusions. Complementary translational and cross-tumor studies inform antigen selection, vaccine delivery, and combination strategies, with uncertain transferability to HPV-negative HNSCC. We examine the separate evidentiary requirements for predicted targets, natural human leukocyte antigen (HLA) presentation, T-cell recognition, tumor-cell killing, and clinical benefit. We discuss spatial immune profiling to investigate resistance and circulating tumor DNA to monitor disease, while emphasizing that their roles in guiding vaccination remain exploratory. Future studies should define the treatment setting, evaluate whether vaccination adds benefit to contemporary standard treatment, report manufacturing attrition and time to first dose, and connect standardized immune measurements with clinical outcomes. Personalized neoepitope vaccination remains investigational in HPV-negative HNSCC, and adequately powered randomized trials are required to establish its clinical role.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the use of neoepitope-based cancer vaccines in human papillomavirus (HPV)-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). The central question is whether these vaccines can improve outcomes in HPV-negative HNSCC, given the substantial risk of recurrence despite multimodal treatment.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with a specific emphasis on the TG4050 randomized phase I study in 33 patients with resected HPV-negative HNSCC. The study supports the feasibility and immunogenicity of personalized neoepitope vaccination but lacks sufficient data to draw definitive efficacy conclusions due to its small sample size, few recurrence events, and per-protocol analysis. The review also highlights the need for adequately powered randomized trials to establish the clinical role of neoepitope vaccination in HPV-negative HNSCC.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that mutation-derived neoepitopes offer a rationale for personalized vaccination in HPV-negative HNSCC, but their clinical relevance depends on factors such as target expression, clonality, antigen presentation, and T-cell functionality. It also emphasizes that while complementary translational and cross-tumor studies provide insights into antigen selection, vaccine delivery, and combination strategies, their transferability to HPV-negative HNSCC remains uncertain.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited evidence on the efficacy of neoepitope-based vaccines in HPV-negative HNSCC, with the TG4050 study being too small to provide conclusive results. Additionally, the review notes that the roles of spatial immune profiling and circulating tumor DNA in guiding vaccination are still exploratory. The review also highlights the need for more research on the treatment setting, the added benefit of vaccination to standard therapies, and the connection between immune measurements and clinical outcomes.
Research and practice implications
Future studies should define the treatment setting, evaluate whether vaccination adds benefit to contemporary standard treatment, report manufacturing attrition and time to first dose, and connect standardized immune measurements with clinical outcomes. The review also suggests that spatial immune profiling and circulating tumor DNA monitoring could be valuable tools in understanding resistance and disease progression, though their roles in guiding vaccination remain under investigation.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区的 HPV 感染:循环基因型、疫苗接种 策略(单剂与两剂)以及 HIV 合并感染的影响

HPV Infection in Sub-Saharan Africa: Circulating Genotypes, Vaccination Strategies (Single-Dose Versus Two-Dose), and Implications of HIV Co-Infection
作者： Margaret Gurumani, Vinie Kouamou, Tariro Makadzange, Azure Tariro Makadzange
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌是撒哈拉以南非洲女性中导致发病率和患病率的主要原因,而在该地区疫苗接种的接种率仍低于世界卫生组织设定的目标,即90%的女孩在15岁前完成完全疫苗接种。本叙述性综述综合了关于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型分布、单剂与双剂疫苗接种接种策略以及人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)共感染影响的证据。尽管HPV 16和HPV 18仍然是主要的致癌型,但HPV 35、HPV 52、HPV 45、HPV 33和HPV 58在撒哈拉以南非洲人群中被频繁报告。因此,一些高危感染类型超出了二价和四价疫苗的覆盖范围,而某些检测到的类型仍不在九价疫苗的覆盖范围内。坦桑尼亚和肯尼亚的随机对照试验显示,单剂疫苗接种在60个月内具有持久的免疫原性,且在54个月内对持续性疫苗型感染的疫苗有效性超过98%。哥斯达黎加的一项试验提供了直接的五年随机对照比较,显示单剂与双剂在预防效果上具有非劣性。对于HIV感染者而言,关于单剂疫苗接种在预防效果和持久性方面的等效证据尚不可用。这些发现支持在免疫功能正常人群中扩大单剂疫苗接种接种,同时在HIV感染者中维持多剂接种路径,采用基因型特异性监测,并继续进行宫颈癌筛查。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),具体分析了在撒哈拉以南非洲地区流行的基因型、疫苗接种接种策略(单剂与双剂)以及HIV共感染的影响。核心问题是:在免疫功能正常人群和HIV感染者中,单剂疫苗接种是否与双剂接种方案在预防HPV相关疾病方面同样有效。
证据基础与方法
本综述是现有证据的叙述性综合,基于在坦桑尼亚、肯尼亚和哥斯达黎加进行的随机对照试验。它包括关于HPV基因型分布、疫苗疫苗有效性和免疫原性的研究。综述未报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是依赖直接比较和观察性数据。
共识与结论
HPV 16和HPV 18仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区的主要致癌型,尽管其他高危型如HPV 35、HPV 52、HPV 45、HPV 33和HPV 58也被频繁报告。在坦桑尼亚和肯尼亚的试验中,单剂疫苗接种已显示出在60个月内具有持久的免疫原性,并且在54个月内对持续性疫苗型感染的有效性超过98%。哥斯达黎加的一项试验也发现,单剂与双剂在五年内的预防效果具有非劣性。
争议与缺口
对于HIV感染者而言,关于单剂HPV 疫苗接种在预防效果和持久性方面的直接证据仍然缺乏。虽然免疫原性和有效性数据在免疫功能正常人群中已有,但由于缺乏针对特定类型的试验,这些发现不能直接应用于HIV感染者。此外,一些高危型HPV感染类型超出了二价和四价疫苗的覆盖范围,而某些类型仍不在九价疫苗的覆盖范围内。
科研与实践启示
这些发现表明,应在免疫功能正常人群中扩大单剂疫苗接种接种,同时在HIV感染者中维持多剂接种路径。需要针对特定基因型进行监测,并继续进行宫颈癌筛查,以弥补疫苗覆盖范围的不足,并监测该地区疫苗接种策略的有效性。

HPV Infection in Sub-Saharan Africa: Circulating Genotypes, Vaccination Strategies (Single-Dose Versus Two-Dose), and Implications of HIV Co-Infection

Authors: Margaret Gurumani, Vinie Kouamou, Tariro Makadzange, Azure Tariro Makadzange
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a major cause of morbidity and mortality among women in sub-Saharan Africa, where vaccination coverage remains below the World Health Organization target of 90% of girls fully vaccinated by 15 years of age. This narrative review synthesizes evidence on human papillomavirus (HPV) genotype distribution, single-dose versus two-dose vaccination, and the implications of human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) co-infection. Although HPV 16 and HPV 18 remain the predominant oncogenic types, HPV 35, HPV 52, HPV 45, HPV 33, and HPV 58 are reported frequently in sub-Saharan African populations. Consequently, some high-risk infections fall outside bivalent and quadrivalent vaccine coverage, and some detected types remain outside nonavalent vaccine coverage. Randomized trials in Tanzania and Kenya demonstrate durable single-dose immunogenicity through 60 months and vaccine efficacy above 98% against persistent vaccine-type infection through 54 months, respectively. A trial in Costa Rica provides a direct five-year randomized comparison showing non-inferiority of one dose to two doses. Equivalent single-dose efficacy and durability evidence for people living with HIV remains unavailable. These findings support expanded single-dose vaccination in immunocompetent populations alongside multidose pathways for people living with HIV, genotype-specific surveillance, and continued cervical screening.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomaviruses (HPV), specifically examining circulating genotypes, vaccination strategies (single-dose versus two-dose), and the implications of HIV co-infection in sub-Saharan Africa. The central question is whether single-dose vaccination is as effective as two-dose regimens in preventing HPV-related diseases, particularly in immunocompetent populations and those living with HIV.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing evidence, drawing on randomized trials conducted in Tanzania, Kenya, and Costa Rica. It includes studies on HPV genotype distribution, vaccine efficacy, and immunogenicity. The review does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but relies on direct comparisons and observational data.
Consensus and conclusions
HPV 16 and HPV 18 remain the predominant oncogenic types in sub-Saharan Africa, although other high-risk types such as HPV 35, HPV 52, HPV 45, HPV 33, and HPV 58 are also frequently reported. Single-dose vaccination has demonstrated durable immunogenicity and high efficacy against persistent vaccine-type infections, with evidence from trials in Tanzania and Kenya showing efficacy above 98% over 54 months. A Costa Rican trial also found non-inferiority of one dose compared to two doses over five years.
Controversies and gaps
There is a lack of direct evidence on the efficacy and durability of single-dose HPV vaccination in people living with HIV. While immunogenicity and efficacy data are available for immunocompetent populations, these findings cannot be directly transferred to HIV-positive individuals due to the absence of target-specific studies. Additionally, some high-risk HPV infections fall outside the coverage of bivalent and quadrivalent vaccines, and certain types remain outside nonavalent vaccine coverage.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest that single-dose vaccination should be expanded in immunocompetent populations, while multidose vaccination pathways should be maintained for people living with HIV. There is a need for genotype-specific surveillance and continued cervical screening to address the gaps in vaccine coverage and to monitor the effectiveness of vaccination strategies in the region.

伊朗成年人中人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种决定因素:使用伊朗HPV 疫苗决定因素量表(I-HPV-VDS)进行的全国代表性调查

Determinants of human papillomavirus vaccine uptake among ​​Iranian adults: a nationally representative survey using the Iranian HPV ​Vaccine Determinants Scale (I-HPV-VDS)
作者： Mehrdad Askarian, Parisa Zare, Mehrdad Vossoughi, Ehsan Taherifard, Nahid Hatam, Rosa Moazed, Mohammad Hossein Taghrir
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究调查了伊朗成年人中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种的决定因素,重点关注影响接受率和覆盖率的因素。核心问题是识别疫苗接种的关键决定因素,并了解当前HPV 疫苗接种在人群中的状况。
证据基础与方法
本次回顾是一项于2025年进行的横断面研究,通过在线调查伊朗成年人完成。研究设计包括问卷的心理测量评估,包括表面效度、结构效度和Cronbach’s alpha。应用事后分层加权以提高人口代表性。采用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来识别HPV 疫苗接种的决定因素。
共识与结论
在加权样本中,有1416名伊朗成年人,HPV 疫苗接种率为5.5%。与较高接种率显著相关的因素包括较年轻年龄、女性性别和未婚状态。此外,较高的社会支持和自我效能水平与疫苗接种率增加相关,而风险感知也显示出正相关。
争议与缺口
研究指出,在多变量分析中,与污名相关的和行为担忧成分并未独立与疫苗接种相关。某些亚组的宽置信区间表明,由于特定层面上事件数量非常少,结合事后分层加权,需要谨慎解释这些亚组特定的结果。
科研与实践启示
HPV 疫苗接种接种率极低,尤其是在男性中,这表明需要采取措施提高接种可及性,例如将HPV 疫苗纳入国家免疫计划。加强公众对HPV易感性和疫苗有效性的理解,并在伊朗背景下改善支持性社会环境,对于提高HPV 疫苗接种接种率至关重要。

Determinants of human papillomavirus vaccine uptake among ​​Iranian adults: a nationally representative survey using the Iranian HPV ​Vaccine Determinants Scale (I-HPV-VDS)

Authors: Mehrdad Askarian, Parisa Zare, Mehrdad Vossoughi, Ehsan Taherifard, Nahid Hatam, Rosa Moazed, Mohammad Hossein Taghrir
Original abstract (English)
Human ​papillomavirus (HPV) infection represents a major global public health burden. Despite the availability of effective vaccines, clear national policies on HPV vaccination remain absent in Iran. Understanding current HPV vaccine coverage and the factors influencing vaccine acceptance among Iranian adults is essential for guiding effective public health interventions. This cross-sectional study was conducted in 2025 using an online survey of Iranian adults. We utilized the newly developed Iranian HPV Vaccine Determinants Scale (I-HPV-VDS), which was constructed based on well-established health behavior frameworks. Psychometric assessment of the questionnaire included using face validity, construct validity, and Cronbach’s alpha. Post-stratification weighting was applied using national distributions of age, gender and educational attainment to improve population representativeness. Univariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used to identify determinants of HPV vaccine uptake. In the weighted sample of 1416 Iranian adults, HPV vaccine uptake was 5.5%. Vaccine uptake was significantly higher among younger adults (aOR: 2.88, 95% CI: 1.52–5.47), females (aOR: 5.90, 95% CI: 3.09–11.26), and unmarried individuals (aOR: 16.72, 95% CI: 7.64–36.57), particularly those previously married or in non-marital relationships. Each one standard deviation increase in Social Support and Self-efficacy was associated with nearly 4 times higher odds of uptake (aOR: 4.46, 95% CI: 2.43–8.18), while Risk Perception was associated with about 2 times higher odds (aOR: 2.12, 95% CI: 1.27–3.52). Interestingly, stigma-related and behavioral concern components were not independently associated with vaccination in multivariable analysis. It is of note that the wide CIs observed for several subgroups likely reflect very low event counts in specific strata combined with post-stratification weighting, which warrants cautious interpretation of these findings. The critically low HPV vaccination uptake, particularly in men, alongside profound social inequities suggest a need to consider measures to improve access, including possible inclusion of the HPV vaccine in the nation​al immunization program. Furthermore, considering recent HPV vaccination initiatives in neighboring countries and the central role of HPV vaccination in global cervical cancer elimination efforts, strengthening public understanding of HPV susceptibility and vaccine effectiveness, as well as enhancing supportive social environments within the Iranian context is essential.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates determinants of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine uptake among Iranian adults, focusing on factors influencing acceptance and coverage. The central question is to identify the key determinants of vaccine uptake and understand the current status of HPV vaccination in the population.
Evidence base and method
The review is a cross-sectional study conducted in 2025 using an online survey of Iranian adults. The study design involved psychometric assessment of the questionnaire, including face validity, construct validity, and Cronbach’s alpha. Post-stratification weighting was applied to enhance population representativeness. Univariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used to identify determinants of HPV vaccine uptake.
Consensus and conclusions
The weighted sample of 1416 Iranian adults showed an HPV vaccine uptake of 5.5%. Factors significantly associated with higher uptake included younger age, female gender, and unmarried status. Additionally, higher levels of Social Support and Self-efficacy were linked to increased odds of vaccine uptake, while Risk Perception also showed a positive association.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that stigma-related and behavioral concern components were not independently associated with vaccination in multivariable analysis. The wide confidence intervals for several subgroups suggest caution in interpreting subgroup-specific findings due to low event counts and post-stratification weighting.
Research and practice implications
The critically low HPV vaccination uptake, especially among men, highlights the need for measures to improve access, such as including the HPV vaccine in the national immunization program. Strengthening public understanding of HPV susceptibility and vaccine effectiveness, along with creating supportive social environments, is essential for enhancing vaccination rates in the Iranian context.

基于CRISPR/Cas的点对点筛查人乳头瘤病毒在宫颈癌中的应用

CRISPR/Cas-Enabled Point-of-Care Screening of Human Papillomavirus in Cervical Cancer
作者： Ke-Xin Jin, Xin-Yue You, Guo-Zhen Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)持续感染,特别是涉及高危亚型的感染,仍然是宫颈癌的主要驱动因素,突显了对快速、可及的点对点(POC)诊断方法的迫切需求。基于CRISPR/Cas的生物传感器因其卓越的特异性和可编程切割活性,已成为点对点检测(POCT)的变革性工具。本综述系统评估了HPV生物标志物,包括DNA、RNA和蛋白质,重点探讨了基于CRISPR/Cas技术的点对点HPV检测方法。我们讨论了多种CRISPR/Cas系统在点对点环境中用于多标志物检测的性能。认识到信号读取在POCT中的关键作用,我们探讨了多种信号增强策略,包括核酸扩增、无需预扩增的检测方法以及针对POCT实用性的集成平台,旨在平衡准确性和实用性。最后,我们讨论了临床转化的主要障碍,并提出了一条道路图,强调人工智能辅助平台在推进多标志物检测中的新兴作用,从而支持未来的去中心化HPV筛查,并有助于全球消除宫颈癌。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),特别是高危亚型,作为与宫颈癌相关的首要病原体。核心问题是评估基于CRISPR/Cas的点对点(POC)HPV检测技术,特别强调其在诊断与宫颈癌相关的疾病如宫颈上皮内瘤变和HPV相关宫颈癌中的应用价值。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对HPV生物标志物的系统性评估,包括DNA、RNA和蛋白质,重点探讨基于CRISPR/Cas技术的点对点检测方法。它讨论了多种CRISPR/Cas系统在点对点环境中用于多标志物检测的性能,并探讨了信号增强策略以平衡准确性和实用性。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,基于CRISPR/Cas的生物传感器由于其卓越的特异性和可编程切割活性,已成为点对点检测的变革性工具。它强调了这些技术在临床环境中快速、可及的HPV筛查潜力,特别是对于与宫颈癌相关的高危亚型。
争议与缺口
综述指出了当前证据的局限性,包括需要在多样化的点对点环境中进一步验证CRISPR/Cas系统,以及缺乏标准化的信号读取协议。它还指出,在资源匮乏环境中,这些技术的性能数据尚不全面。
科研与实践启示
综述提出了临床转化的道路图,强调整合人工智能辅助平台以增强多标志物检测能力。它建议未来研究优先考虑开发更稳健、更用户友好的点对点系统,并提高其在去中心化医疗环境中的适用性。}}.{

CRISPR/Cas-Enabled Point-of-Care Screening of Human Papillomavirus in Cervical Cancer

Authors: Ke-Xin Jin, Xin-Yue You, Guo-Zhen Liu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) persistent infection, particularly involving high-risk subtypes, remains the primary driver of cervical cancer, underlining the exigent need for rapid, accessible point-of-care (POC) diagnostics. CRISPR/Cas-based biosensors have developed as transformative tools for POC testing (POCT) because of their exceptional specificity and programmable cleavage activity. This review systematically evaluates HPV biomarkers, including DNA, RNA, and proteins, with a focus on CRISPR/Cas-enabled technologies for POC detection of HPV. We discuss the performance of diverse CRISPR/Cas systems for multiplex biomarker detection in POC settings for HPV infection screening. Recognizing the critical role of signal readout in POCT, we explore several signal-enhancement strategies, including nucleic acid amplification, preamplification-free assays, and integrated platforms tailored to POCT practicality, all aimed at balancing accuracy and practicality. Finally, we address key barriers to clinical translation and propose a roadmap that highlights the emerging role of AI-assisted platforms in advancing multiplexed detection, thereby supporting future decentralized HPV screening and contributing to the global elimination of cervical cancer.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Human papillomaviruses (HPV), particularly high-risk subtypes, as the primary pathogen associated with cervical cancer. The central question is the evaluation of CRISPR/Cas-enabled technologies for point-of-care (POC) detection of HPV, with an emphasis on their utility in diagnosing HPV-related diseases such as cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic evaluation of HPV biomarkers, including DNA, RNA, and proteins, with a focus on CRISPR/Cas-based technologies for POC detection. It discusses the performance of diverse CRISPR/Cas systems for multiplex biomarker detection in POC settings and explores signal-enhancement strategies to balance accuracy and practicality.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CRISPR/Cas-based biosensors have emerged as transformative tools for POC testing due to their exceptional specificity and programmable cleavage activity. It highlights the potential of these technologies for rapid, accessible HPV screening in clinical settings, particularly for high-risk subtypes associated with cervical cancer.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies limitations in the current evidence, including the need for further validation of CRISPR/Cas systems in diverse POC settings and the lack of standardized protocols for signal readout. It also notes the absence of comprehensive data on the performance of these technologies in low-resource environments.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes a roadmap for clinical translation, emphasizing the integration of AI-assisted platforms to enhance multiplexed detection capabilities. It suggests future research priorities in developing more robust, user-friendly POC systems and improving their applicability in decentralized healthcare settings.

人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关口腔黏膜乳头状病变:一种关于临床、组织病理学和基因型特异性特征的叙述性综述

Human Papillomavirus-Associated Papillomatous Lesions of the Oral Mucosa: A Narrative Review of Clinical, Histopathological, and Genotype-Specific Features
作者： Gabriela Boldeanu, Ilona Mihaela Liliac, Elena Cristina ANDREI, Cristian Niky Cumpătă, C. Patru, Larisa Pătru, Mihaela Jana Țuculină, Oana Andreea Diaconu, Oprea Valentin Bușu, Cătălina Alexandra Iacov
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)可引起一系列上皮病变,从短暂感染和良性乳头状增生到由HPV驱动的肿瘤。在口腔内,几种乳头状病变在形态学上存在重叠,但其基因型分布和临床意义有所不同。本叙述性综述综合了这些病变的临床、组织病理学和分子证据,并根据每项研究提供的病毒证据水平对每个主要研究进行分级,从临床HPV到转录活性感染。HPV 6和11主要与HPV阳性尖锐湿疣和鳞状乳头状瘤相关,而HPV 13和32则与多灶性上皮增生相关;HPV 2和4与口腔寻常疣的关联基于少数研究;许多鳞状乳头状瘤为HPV阴性。与转录活性HPV相关的口腔上皮异型增生是一种独特的潜在恶性病变,其发生频率和转化风险仍不确定。koilocytosis、乳头状结构、p16表达和HPV DNA检测本身并不能确立因果关系,且口咽癌的证据不能直接推广到口腔。我们提出了针对不同病变的补充HPV检测指征,并总结了哪些结论有充分支持,哪些仍存在不确定性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)及其与口腔黏膜乳头状病变的关联。它探讨了这些病变的临床、组织病理学和基因型特异性特征,特别强调了HPV类型及其在疾病进展中的作用。核心问题是:HPV相关的口腔上皮异型增生是否代表一种独特的潜在恶性病变,以及不同的HPV基因型如何影响各种口腔病变。
证据基础与方法
本综述是临床、组织病理学和分子证据的叙述性综合。它根据每项研究提供的病毒证据水平对每个主要研究进行分级,从临床HPV到转录活性感染。本综述未进行荟萃分析,而是对文献中的发现进行了定性总结。
共识与结论
综述得出结论:HPV 6和11主要与尖锐湿疣和鳞状乳头状瘤相关,而HPV 13和32则与多灶性上皮增生相关。然而,HPV 2和4与口腔寻常疣的关联基于有限的研究。此外,HPV相关的口腔上皮异型增生被认为是一种独特的潜在恶性病变,但其发生频率和转化风险仍不确定。
争议与缺口
关于使用koilocytosis、乳头状结构、p16表达和HPV DNA检测等标志物来确立因果关系存在争议。综述还指出,口咽癌的证据不能直接推广到口腔。此外,HPV相关口腔上皮异型增生的发生频率和转化风险尚未明确,表明存在显著的证据缺口。
科研与实践启示
综述提出了针对不同病变的补充HPV检测指征,并强调了区分有充分支持的结论与仍不确定的结论的必要性。它建议需要进一步研究以明确特定HPV基因型在口腔病变中的作用,并更好地理解HPV相关口腔上皮异型增生的转化风险。

Human Papillomavirus-Associated Papillomatous Lesions of the Oral Mucosa: A Narrative Review of Clinical, Histopathological, and Genotype-Specific Features

Authors: Gabriela Boldeanu, Ilona Mihaela Liliac, Elena Cristina ANDREI, Cristian Niky Cumpătă, C. Patru, Larisa Pătru, Mihaela Jana Țuculină, Oana Andreea Diaconu, Oprea Valentin Bușu, Cătălina Alexandra Iacov
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes a spectrum of epithelial lesions, from transient infection and benign papillary proliferations to HPV-driven neoplasia. In the oral cavity, several papillomatous lesions share overlapping morphology but differ in genotype distribution and clinical significance. This narrative review synthesizes clinical, histopathological, and molecular evidence on these lesions and grades each primary study by the level of viral evidence it provides, from clinical diagnosis to transcriptionally active infection. HPV 6 and 11 predominate in HPV-positive condylomas and squamous papillomas and HPV 13 and 32 in multifocal epithelial hyperplasia, whereas the association of HPV 2 and 4 with oral verruca vulgaris rests on few studies; many squamous papillomas are HPV-negative. HPV-associated oral epithelial dysplasia, linked to transcriptionally active high-risk HPV, is a distinct potentially malignant entity whose frequency and transformation risk remain uncertain. Koilocytosis, papillary architecture, p16 expression, and HPV DNA detection do not by themselves establish causation, and evidence from oropharyngeal carcinoma cannot be extended to the oral cavity. We propose lesion-specific indications for supplementary HPV testing and summarize which conclusions are well supported and which remain uncertain.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on human papillomaviruses (HPV) and their association with papillomatous lesions in the oral mucosa. It examines the clinical, histopathological, and genotype-specific features of these lesions, with an emphasis on HPV types and their role in disease progression. The central question is whether HPV-associated oral epithelial dysplasia represents a distinct potentially malignant entity and how different HPV genotypes contribute to various oral lesions.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of clinical, histopathological, and molecular evidence. It grades each primary study based on the level of viral evidence it provides, ranging from clinical diagnosis to transcriptionally active infection. The review does not perform a meta-analysis but instead provides a qualitative summary of findings from the literature.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HPV 6 and 11 are predominantly associated with condylomas and squamous papillomas, while HPV 13 and 32 are linked to multifocal epithelial hyperplasia. However, the association of HPV 2 and 4 with oral verruca vulgaris is based on limited studies. Additionally, HPV-associated oral epithelial dysplasia is considered a distinct potentially malignant entity, though its frequency and transformation risk remain uncertain.
Controversies and gaps
There is controversy regarding the use of markers such as koilocytosis, papillary architecture, p16 expression, and HPV DNA detection to establish causation. The review also highlights that evidence from oropharyngeal carcinoma cannot be directly extended to the oral cavity. Furthermore, the frequency and transformation risk of HPV-associated oral epithelial dysplasia are not well established, indicating a significant evidence gap.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes lesion-specific indications for supplementary HPV testing and emphasizes the need to distinguish between well-supported conclusions and those that remain uncertain. It suggests that further research is required to clarify the role of specific HPV genotypes in oral lesions and to better understand the transformation risk of HPV-associated oral epithelial dysplasia.

Buschke-Löwenstein肿瘤谱系:一名青少年自身免疫性溶血性贫血(AIHA)患者合并高危型HPV与低危型HPV混合感染的巨大型尖锐湿疣:一例病例报告

Buschke-Löwenstein Tumor Spectrum Giant Condyloma with Mixed High- and Low-Risk Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Coinfection in an Adolescent with Autoimmune Hemolytic Anemia (AIHA): A Case Report.
作者： Dewi Rani Pelitawati, Deby Johor Hasan, Pati Aji Achdiat, Dodi Suardi
摘要中文翻译背景:Buschke-Löwenstein肿瘤(BLT),或称为巨大型尖锐湿疣(GCA),是一种罕见的、局部侵袭性的与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关的泌尿生殖器病变,其特征是广泛的疣状增生,并具有已知的恶性转化潜力。尽管BLT传统上与低危型HPV基因型6和11相关,但同时存在的高危型致癌基因型可能会增加致癌潜力,尤其是在免疫功能低下的个体中。目的:报告一例罕见的BLT谱系巨大型尖锐湿疣病例,该病例发生于一名患有自身免疫性溶血性贫血(AIHA)的青少年女孩,并伴有混合型HPV 11、16和18感染,讨论其相关的诊断挑战及从低资源环境视角出发的潜在致癌风险。病例报告:一名16岁的HIV阴性、未婚青少年,因8个月内逐渐增大的外阴肿块就诊,其中最近两个月增长显著。体格检查发现一个疼痛明显的大型疣状尖锐湿疣样肿块,大小为15 × 5 × 3 cm,覆盖阴道口。该病变未阻塞尿道口。她还伴有严重的AIHA,需要糖皮质激素治疗和输血支持。在多学科团队稳定病情后,进行了外阴、阴道和宫颈病变的切除和烧灼术,并在手术过程中使用尿管作为标志物以保护尿道。组织病理学显示乳头状增生的复层鳞状上皮,伴有显著的棘层增厚、过度角化、延长的乳头状突起以及明显的空泡样细胞变化,符合HPV相关的细胞病变效应,未见间质浸润或恶性转化。免疫组织化学检查显示Ki-67增殖活性增加,p16呈斑片状(非块状)表达,p53呈野生型,符合与HPV相关的活跃病变,但未见免疫表型证据显示转录活性高危型HPV或浸润性癌。HPV基因分型检测发现HPV 11、16和18,而HPV 6为阴性。结论:该病例展示了在免疫调节异常的青少年中,BLT谱系巨大型尖锐湿疣的诊断复杂性。尽管组织病理学结果为良性,但同时存在高危型HPV基因型、快速生长以及免疫功能损害,提示需要谨慎评估潜在的癌前病变风险,并强调了充分的组织取样、长期监测以及HPV预防的重要性。这些结论基于单一病例,应据此进行解读。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
Buschke-Löwenstein肿瘤(BLT),或称为巨大型尖锐湿疣(GCA),是一种罕见的、局部侵袭性的与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关的肛周生殖器病变,其特征为广泛的疣状增生,并具有已知的恶性转化潜力。本文旨在报告一例青少年女性患者合并自身免疫性溶血性贫血(AIHA)及混合型HPV 11、16和18感染的BLT谱系巨大型尖锐湿疣病例,并探讨其相关的诊断挑战及来自低资源环境视角的潜在致癌风险。
证据基础与方法
病例报告:一名16岁、HIV阴性且未婚的青少年,近8个月来出现外阴肿块逐渐增大,最近两个月肿块显著增大。
共识与结论
组织病理学显示乳头状增生性复层鳞状上皮,伴有显著的棘层增厚、角化过度、延长的乳头状沟和明显的空泡化细胞改变,与人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)相关细胞病变效应一致,未见间质浸润或恶性转化。免疫组织化学检查显示Ki-67增殖活性增加,斑片状(非块状)p16表达,以及野生型p53,符合与HPV相关的活跃病变,无免疫表型证据显示高危型HPV转录活性或浸润性癌。HPV分型检测发现HPV 11、16和18型,而HPV 6型为阴性。
争议与缺口
这些结论基于单一病例,应据此进行解释。
科研与实践启示
尽管组织病理学表现良性,但高危型HPV基因型共存、快速增殖以及免疫学损害提示需要谨慎解读潜在的癌前风险,并强调了充分的组织取样、长期监测监测以及HPV预防的重要性。

Buschke-Löwenstein Tumor Spectrum Giant Condyloma with Mixed High- and Low-Risk Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Coinfection in an Adolescent with Autoimmune Hemolytic Anemia (AIHA): A Case Report.

Authors: Dewi Rani Pelitawati, Deby Johor Hasan, Pati Aji Achdiat, Dodi Suardi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Buschke-Löwenstein tumor (BLT), or giant condyloma acuminatum (GCA) is a rare, locally aggressive HPV-associated anogenital lesion characterized by extensive verrucous proliferation and a recognized potential for malignant transformation. Although BLT is classically linked to low-risk HPV genotypes 6 and 11, coexisting high-risk oncogenic genotypes may increase oncogenic potential, particularly in immunocompromised individuals. PURPOSE: To report a rare case of BLT-spectrum giant condyloma in an adolescent girl with autoimmune hemolytic anemia (AIHA) and mixed HPV 11, 16, and 18 coinfection, and to discuss the associated diagnostic challenges and potential oncogenic risk from a low resource setting perspective. CASE REPORT: A 16-year-old HIV-negative, unmarried adolescent presented with a progressively enlarging vulvar mass over 8 months, with marked growth over the preceding two months. Physical examination revealed a painful large verrucous condylomatous mass measuring 15 × 5 × 3 cm, covering the vaginal introitus. The lesion did not obstruct the urethral meatus. She also had severe AIHA requiring corticosteroid therapy and transfusion support. After multidisciplinary stabilization, excision and cauterization of vulvar, vaginal, and cervical lesions were performed, with the urethra protected intraoperatively using urinary catheter as a landmark. Histopathology showed papillomatous hyperplastic stratified squamous epithelium with marked acanthosis, hyperkeratosis, elongated rete ridges, and prominent koilocytotic changes, consistent with HPV-associated cytopathic effects, without stromal invasion or malignant transformation. Immunohistochemistry demonstrated increased Ki-67 proliferative activity, patchy (non-block) p16, and wild-type p53, consistent with a productive HPV-associated lesion without immunophenotypic evidence of transcriptionally active high-risk HPV or invasive carcinoma. HPV genotyping detected HPV 11, 16, and 18, whereas HPV 6 was negative. CONCLUSION: This case illustrates the diagnostic complexity of BLT-spectrum giant condyloma in adolescent with immune dysregulation. Despite benign histopathology, coexistence of high-risk HPV genotypes, rapid growth, and immunological impairment warrants cautious interpretation regarding potential premalignant risk and underscores the importance of adequate tissue sampling, long-term surveillance, and HPV prevention. These conclusions are based on a single case and should be interpreted accordingly.

Scope and question
Buschke-Löwenstein tumor (BLT), or giant condyloma acuminatum (GCA) is a rare, locally aggressive HPV-associated anogenital lesion characterized by extensive verrucous proliferation and a recognized potential for malignant transformation. To report a rare case of BLT-spectrum giant condyloma in an adolescent girl with autoimmune hemolytic anemia (AIHA) and mixed HPV 11, 16, and 18 coinfection, and to discuss the associated diagnostic challenges and potential oncogenic risk from a low resource setting perspective.

Consensus and conclusions

Immunohistochemistry demonstrated increased Ki-67 proliferative activity, patchy (non-block) p16, and wild-type p53, consistent with a productive HPV-associated lesion without immunophenotypic evidence of transcriptionally active high-risk HPV or invasive carcinoma. HPV genotyping detected HPV 11, 16, and 18, whereas HPV 6 was negative.

These conclusions are based on a single case and should be interpreted accordingly.

Association of HPV Infection with Vaginal Microecology and Abnormal Cervical Cytology: Dose–Response Effects of Viral Load in a Cross-Sectional Study of 7,997 Women in Tianmen, China

Association of HPV Infection with Vaginal Microecology and Abnormal Cervical Cytology: Dose–Response Effects of Viral Load in a Cross-Sectional Study of 7,997 Women in Tianmen, China
作者： Meijun Chen, Huashun Li, Weiwei Cao, Tingting Ma, Feibo Guo

Association of HPV Infection with Vaginal Microecology and Abnormal Cervical Cytology: Dose–Response Effects of Viral Load in a Cross-Sectional Study of 7,997 Women in Tianmen, China
